# Common Settings - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#common-settings)

Common Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#common-settings "Permanent link")

=====================================================================================================================

Many tools share common options. They are listed on this page for reference.

*   [Mask Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
    
*   [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
    
*   [Even Thickness](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness)
    

* * *

Mask Falloff[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

When two sets of data blend together there are often falloff controls. These can be found across normalMagic in tools such as [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
, [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 and [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
.

### Masking Type[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#masking-type "Permanent link")

#### Boundary Edge Expand[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#boundary-edge-expand "Permanent link")

Select vertices on the boundary edge and expand selection inwards with a linear falloff.

*   **Expand Iterations:** How many iterations to expand mask inwards.

[![Expand Boundary](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/expand_boundary.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/expand_boundary.gif)

#### Boundary Edge Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#boundary-edge-distance "Permanent link")

Select vertices based on their distance to a boundary edge with a linear falloff.

*   **Start Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 1.
*   **End Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 0.

[![Boundary Distance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/boundary_distance.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/boundary_distance.gif)

#### Surface Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#surface-distance "Permanent link")

Select vertices based on their distance to the target geometry.

*   **Start Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 1.
*   **End Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 0.

[![Target Distance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/surface_falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/surface_falloff.gif)

### Base Falloff[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#base-falloff "Permanent link")

The falloff used for values between 0-1. Here it is being used in [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
:

[![Base Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/base_falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/base_falloff.gif)

### Falloff Curve[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#falloff-curve "Permanent link")

Bias the selection towards 1. Here it is being used in [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
:

[![Falloff Curve](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/falloff_curve.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/falloff_curve.gif)

* * *

Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Often we'll want to limit the effects of modifiers to certain areas. The "Selection" section is the same for many tools. It allows for partial selection and, when normals are stored on face corners, hard boundaries.

*   **Vertex Group:** Specify a vertex group to limit modifier's effect.
*   **Face Group (Face Corner Selection Only):** Specify a vertex group that will have hard boundaries.
*   **Material:** Limit the modifier's effect to a specific material.
*   **Invert:** Invert selection.

Here we can see how **Vertex Group** and **Face Group** can be combined:

[![Selection Demo](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/selection.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/selection/selection.gif)

* * *

Even Thickness[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Many operations involve offsetting the surface of a mesh. These settings will determine how the offset is calculated.

*   [![offset_even](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/offset_even.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/offset_even.gif)
    
*   *   **None:** Points are offset the same distance regardless of face angles.
    *   **Simple:** Distance of offset is limited to attempt even thickness with neighboring faces.
    *   **Constraints:** Distance of offset is projected to attempt even thickness with neighboring faces.

### Constraints Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#constraints-options "Permanent link")

*   **Clamp Multiplier:** Limit offset distance to a multiple of specified distance. This keeps "explosion error" limited.
*   **Boundary:** How to offset points on the mesh boundary:
    
    *   **Normal:** No change in offset direction.
    *   **Flat:** Flatten the offset direction at mesh boundaries.
    *   **Boundary Angle:** Make offset perpendicular to angle of boundary edges. This exposes a setting for how many edges to check to gain an average boundary angle.
    
    See the "Constraint Boundary Shape" Solidify Pro example in the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/#solidify-pro)
    . [![boundary_shape](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/boundary_shape.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/boundary_shape.png)
    .

# Tools Overview - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/tools_overview/#tools-overview)

Tools Overview[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/tools_overview/#tools-overview "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**. These can be unintuitive to work with if you're unfamiliar with them.

Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it is something to be aware of.

Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/tools_overview/#asset-library "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The core of normalMagic is an asset library. You will find links to all the tools in the sidebar under the following categories:

*   [Mesh Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/)
    
*   [Normal Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/)
    
*   [Visualize Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/)
    
*   [Other Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/)
    
*   [Manual Tools](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/)
    

Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/tools_overview/#add-on "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Included with the Pro version, the add-on makes it easier to set up some of the modifiers.

[Add-on Documentation](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/)

# Licensing - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#licensing)

Licensing[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#licensing "Permanent link")

===================================================================================================

Individual Licenses[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#individual-licenses "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

For an individual purchasing normalMagic, licenses are as follows:

*   Base Version (Asset Library Only): [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
    .
*   Pro Version:
    *   Add-on Python Code: [GNU General Public License](https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html)
        .
    *   Asset Library: [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
        .

Team / Studio Licenses[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#team-studio-licenses "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

For teams/studios the number of people that use normalMagic is limited by the version purchased. The following team sizes are available:

*   Small Studio: up to 9 users.
*   Large Studio: 10-20 users.

For teams that exceed 20 users, additional licenses should be purchased. This only applies to the asset library's [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
. The licensing is as follows:

*   Asset Library: [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
    .
*   Add-on Python Code: [GNU General Public License](https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html)
    .

Early Access Customers

For Studios that bought early access copies: individual licenses can be transferred into a studio license per request. This will discount the studio license by the price paid for each individual license.

Enterprise License[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#enterprise-license "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

For unlimited users across a large organization please reach out via email: spaghetmenot@gmail.com

Standard Royalty Free License[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Standard Royalty Free license grants you, the purchaser, the ability to make use of the purchased product for personal, educational, or commercial purposes as long as those purposes do not violate the following:

*   You may not resell, redistribute, or repackage the purchased product without explicit permission from the original author.

GNU General Public License[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/licensing/#gnu-general-public-license "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Please find the GNU/GPL license here: [https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html](https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html)
.

# Pro Add-on - normalMagic

Pro Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/#pro-add-on "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================

This add-on is included with the **Pro** version of normalMagic. It streamlines workflows and makes working with certain modifiers easier.

    Set up Boolean modifiers with one click:
    
    *   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
        
    *   [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
        
    *   [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
        
    *   [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
        
*   [Modifier References](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_references/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![modifier reference panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_reference_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_reference_panel.png)
     Manage complex non-destructive scenes with many cutters, inserts, and other objects referenced by modifiers.
    
*   [Surface Integration](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![surface integration](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/surface_integration.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/surface_integration.png)
      
    Set up modifiers with one click:
    
    *   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
        
    *   [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
        
*   [Instance Objects](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/instance_objects/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![instance panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)
    
    Duplicate objects as instances using the [Instance Object](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/instance_object/)
     modifier with one click.
    
*   [Object Display](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/object_display/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![object display panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/object_display_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/object_display_panel.png)
     Instantly make objects "Viewport Only", hiding them from all renders.

# Boolean Pro - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro)

Boolean Pro[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro "Permanent link")

================================================================================================================

[![bpro panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)

This panel lets you quickly set up Boolean modifiers on selected geometry. Modifiers are added to the active object, other selected objects will be added as cutters. There are operators for adding the following modifiers:

*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    
*   [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
    
*   [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
    
*   [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
    

*   ### How to use[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#how-to-use "Permanent link")
    
    [![bool diff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/boolean_difference.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/boolean_difference.gif)
    
    **1.** Select cutter object(s).  
    **2.** Select the object you wish to cut.  
    **3.** Perform Boolean operation using the buttons.
    

Boolean Pro Operators[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro-operators "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The top of the panel contains operators for all Boolean Pro modes.

### Operations[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#operations "Permanent link")

| Intersect | Union | Difference | Slice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [![Intersect](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png) | [![Union](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png) | [![Difference](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png) | [![Slice](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif) |

### Split[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#split "Permanent link")

The "Split" Operator will duplicate the object:

*   The original will get a **Difference** Boolean
*   The duplicate will get a **Intersect** Boolean.
*   The duplicate can be a linked copy by toggling the link option next to the button.

Advanced Boolean Operators[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#advanced-boolean-operators "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The "Advanced Booleans" section lets you add the following modifiers:

*   [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
    
*   [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
    
*   [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
    

Modifier Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#modifier-settings "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To change the default settings for any modifier, use the [Modifier Defaults](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/)
 menu to save defaults from a modifier.

Cutter Behavior[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#cutter-behavior "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section lets you choose what happens to objects when you add a Boolean.

*   **Display As.** Change the display mode of the cutter object:
    
    *   **Wireframe.** Display cutter as wireframe.
    *   **Bounds.** Display cutter as bounding box.
*   **Collection.** When enabled, specify the name of a collection to add cutter objects to. If the collection doesn't exist one will be created.
    
*   **Deselect.** Which objects get deselected after the boolean operation:
    *   **None.** Keep all objects selected.
    *   **Main Object.** Deselect the object that receives the modifier.
    *   **Cutters.** Deselect cutter object(s)
*   **Parent to Object.** Parent the cutter object(s) to the main object. This will transform them together when you move the main object.
*   **Link Split Mesh.** When performing the [Split](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#split)
     operation, link the intersected mesh to the original so editing one mesh edits the other too.
*   **Auto-Smooth.** Apply Blender's "Smooth By Angle" modifier to objects in Boolean operation if they don't have one already:
    *   **Cutters.** Apply smoothing to cutter object(s).
    *   **All.** Apply smoothing to all objects involved in the Boolean.
    *   **None.** Don't apply smoothing to any objects.
*   **Angle.** Angle to set auto-smoothing at when "Auto-Smooth" is turned on.

Convert Existing Booleans[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#convert-existing-booleans "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Where is this operator?

There is no longer a button for this operator but you can search for it with the F3 operator search: [![convert booleans](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/convert_booleans.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/convert_booleans.png)

Will convert all 'Boolean' modifiers on selected objects to 'Boolean Pro'. All relevant settings will be transferred.

# Instance Objects - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/instance_objects/#instance-objects)

Instance Objects[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/instance_objects/#instance-objects "Permanent link")

===============================================================================================================================

[![instance panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)

These tools make it easier to use the [Instance Object](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/instance_object/)
 modifier.

Duplicate Instanced[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/instance_objects/#duplicate-instanced "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This operator that acts like Blender's built-in [Duplicate Linked](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/scene_layout/object/editing/duplicate_linked.html)
 but also copies the results of all modifiers:

[![duplicate instanced](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/duplicate_instanced.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/duplicate_instanced.gif)

The **instanced** object can be duplicated with any existing methods e.g. Shift+D and will keep the same behavior.

Using this operator on an object that already is using a "Instance Object" as it's last modifier will instance the original object to keep things simple.

### What it does[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/instance_objects/#what-it-does "Permanent link")

For each selected object:

1.  Create a new object with the same mesh data as selected object. This is so you can still enter "Edit Mode" on the instance.
2.  Add [Instance Object](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/instance_object/)
     modifier and specify selected object.
3.  Select instance object and deselect original.

Select Source[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/instance_objects/#select-source "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Selects the source object(s) being instanced by selected object(s). This is useful when you wish to edit the mesh or change modifiers on the original object.

---

# Modifier Defaults - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/#modifier-defaults)

Modifier Defaults[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/#modifier-defaults "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================================

You can save default settings for any modifier added via normalMagic operators.

Note

This will have no effect on modifiers added through the "Add Modifier" menu.

This is achieved through the **Modifier Defaults** menu, available in the [Modifier Header](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/)
 bar and the [Modifier References](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_references/)
 panel.

[![modifier defaults locations](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_defaults.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_defaults.png)

---

# Modifier Header - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/#modifier-header)

Modifier Header[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/#modifier-header "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================================

[![modifier header](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_header.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_header.png)

The optional modifier header bar gives quick access to several normalMagic features without using the sidebar. From left to right:

### Expand Arrow[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/#expand-arrow "Permanent link")

Show the [Modifier References](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_references/)
 panel below.

### Operators Bar[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/#operators-bar "Permanent link")

A row of buttons for quickly adding normalMagic operators. These can configured in [preferences](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/preferences/)
.

All of these allow for multi-object selection, for assigning cutters for example.

### Modifier Defaults[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/#modifier-defaults "Permanent link")

Saves all the settings for the active modifier. This will be used any time a modifier of the same type is added via the add-on. See [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/)
 for more info.

---

# Modifier References - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_references/#modifier-references)

Modifier References[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_references/#modifier-references "Permanent link")

========================================================================================================================================

Use this panel to manage complex non-destructive scenes with many cutters, inserts, or other objects referenced by modifiers.

Active Object

This panel shows modifiers for the **active object** only i.e. modifiers that show up in Blender's modifiers panel.

[![Modifier Reference Panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_reference_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_reference_panel.png)

Instantly select, show or hide objects that are referenced by the active object's modifiers.

UI Breakdown[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_references/#ui-breakdown "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The main list displays every modifier on the active object that references other objects. Selecting a modifier will also select it in the object's modifier panel and display it's individual references below.

*   [![Modifier Reference Panel Annotated](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_reference_panel_annotated.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifier_reference_panel_annotated.png)
    
*   **1.** **Hide/Show All:** Hide or show all referenced objects for the active object. Filter the modifier list below.
    
    **2.** **Modifier List:** Each entry here is a modifier on the active object:  
    \- **Toggle** modifier  
    \- **Show/Hide** referenced objects  
    \- **Select/Deselect** referenced objects
    
    **3.** **Modifier Defaults:** Set the default settings for the highlighted modifier. See [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/)
     for more information.
    
    **4.** **Apply/Remove Modifier:** Apply or remove the highlighted modifier with an option to delete referenced objects.
    
    **5.** **Referenced Objects:** Displays referenced objects, you can rename them here.

---

# Object Display - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/object_display/#object-display)

Object Display[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/object_display/#object-display "Permanent link")

=========================================================================================================================

[![Object Display Panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/object_display_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/object_display_panel.png)

This panel helps manage object's visibility. Assigning a mesh as **_Viewport Only_** will hide it from all renders but keep it visible in the viewport.

This is particularly useful for objects referenced by [Mesh Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/)
 such as [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)

[![Object Display GIF](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/object_display.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/object_display.gif)

Rendered[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/object_display/#rendered "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Show object in renders and viewport. Applies default "visible" object properties.

Display in viewport as either **Wire**, **Solid** (default), or **Textured**.

Viewport Only[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/object_display/#viewport-only "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Hides object from all renders but keeps it visible in the viewport.

Display in viewport as either **Bounds**, **Wire** (default), or **Solid**.

---

# Preferences - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/preferences/#preferences)

Preferences[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/preferences/#preferences "Permanent link")

================================================================================================================

These options show up under **_Edit/Preferences/Add-ons/normalMagic_**

UI Preferences[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/preferences/#ui-preferences "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Show Sidebar Panel.** Enable/disable the add-on's sidebar panel. Expand to specify it's category (default is "Normal Magic")
*   **Show Modifier Header.** Enable/disable the [modifier header](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/)
    . Expand to choose which modifiers show in the quick bar.

Preferred Smooth Modifier[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/preferences/#preferred-smooth-modifier "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Choose which smooth modifier gets added with Boolean Pro's cutter "Auto-Smooth" option.

Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/preferences/#asset-library "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Checks if asset library exists and lists the path.

*   **Automatic Install:** When no asset library is detected, install the asset library from the add-on (recommended).
*   **Refresh Asset Library Location:** When manually moving the asset library, detect the new path for the add-on to use.
*   **Enable Asset Library:** When a normalMagic asset library exists but is disabled, you can enable from here.

---

# Surface Integration - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#surface-integration)

Surface Integration[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#surface-integration "Permanent link")

========================================================================================================================================

[![surface_integration](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/surface_integration.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/surface_integration.png)

This panel contains operators to quickly add [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 and [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
 modifiers.

Surface Insert[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#surface-insert "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Add a [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 modifier to the active object.

### How to Use[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#how-to-use "Permanent link")

1.  (Optional) Select 1-6 insert meshes
2.  Select surface mesh last
3.  Press the Surface Insert button

### Insert mesh options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#insert-mesh-options "Permanent link")

Similar to the Boolean [cutters](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#cutter-behavior)
 menu, there are options for what to do with insert meshes.

[![insert_mesh_options](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/insert_mesh_options.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/insert_mesh_options.png)

Project to Surface[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#project-to-surface "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Add a [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
 modifier to all selected objects but the last selected.

### How to Use[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#how-to-use_1 "Permanent link")

1.  Select one or more objects you wish to project
2.  Select the surface mesh last
3.  Press the Project to Surface button

### Projection Mesh Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#projection-mesh-options "Permanent link")

Similar to the Boolean [cutters](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#cutter-behavior)
 menu, there are options for what to do with the projected meshes.

[![projection_mesh_options](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/project_to_surface_options.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/project_to_surface_options.png)

Modifier Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/#modifier-settings "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To change default modifier settings for either modifier, see [modifier defaults](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/)
.

[![defaults](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifer_defaults_generic.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/modifer_defaults_generic.png)

# Manual Tools - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/#manual-tools)

Manual Tools[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/#manual-tools "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================

These tools are available as destructive operations in "Edit" mode. There isn't much here yet, if you wish to see more modifiers become manual tools please reach out!

*   [Recalculate Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/recalculate_normals/)

# Add Custom Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/#add-custom-normals)

Add Custom Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/#add-custom-normals "Permanent link")

===========================================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/add_custom_normals_object.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/add_custom_normals_object.png)

Found in the **Object** menu. Adds custom normals to selected mesh objects. Internally uses the [Lock Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/lock_normals/)
 tool.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Type:** How to store custom normals:
    
    *   **Free:** Store custom normals as simple vectors in the local space of the mesh. This is efficient and fast to evaluate but does not support deformation.
    *   **Tangent Space:** Store normals in a deformation dependent custom transformation space. This method is slower, but can be better when subsequent operations change the mesh without handling normals specifically.
*   **Normal Domain:** When **_Custom Normal Type_** is set to **_Free_**:
    
    *   **Face Corner.** Store normals on face corners, allows for sharp edges.
    *   **Point.** Store normals on points, cannot have sharp edges.

---

# Recalculate Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/recalculate_normals/#recalculate-normals)

Recalculate Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/recalculate_normals/#recalculate-normals "Permanent link")

==============================================================================================================================================

[![recalculate_weighted](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/recalculate_normals.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/recalculate_normals.png)

Found in the **Mesh/Normals** alt + N menu in "Edit Mode".

Calculated weighted normals and mark sharp for selected geometry. This is a tool version of the [Face Weighted Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/)
 and [Mark Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/)
 modifiers.

# Mesh Modifiers - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/#mesh-modifiers)

Mesh Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/#mesh-modifiers "Permanent link")

==============================================================================================================

These modifiers affect normals, underlying geometry and other attributes such as UVs.

*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    : Replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier with extra options and accurate normals.
*   [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
    : Cut and 'Trim' a mesh using a single-sided cutter object.
*   [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
    : Cuts and extrudes where cutter meshes intersect.
*   [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    : Projects and blends mesh edges onto other mesh surfaces.
*   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    : Projects, cuts, blends and welds other mesh objects into the surface of a mesh.
*   [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
    : Single axis mirror modifier with extra options and works with custom normals.
*   [Solidify Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/)
    : Solidify Meshes with advanced profile control. Supports custom normals.
*   [Offset Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/offset_surface/)
    : Offset the surface of a mesh with options for even thickness.
*   [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
    :Cuts and extrudes where cutter meshes intersect.

---

# Cut Groove - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#cut-groove)

Cut Groove[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#cut-groove "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================

[![Cut Groove Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/cut_groove_2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/cut_groove_2.png)

Cut a v-shaped groove or a gap into a surface along the intersection of cutter objects.

How it works[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#how-it-works "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The **Cut Groove** Modifier performs the following steps:

1.  Generate a curve from the intersection of cutter objects.
2.  Convert the curve to a 'ribbon' mesh that follows the surface closely.
3.  Extrude the 'ribbon' mesh with a v-shaped profile to create a solid 'groove cutter'.
4.  Cut the 'groove cutter' from the original surface using a difference Boolean.

Viewing Steps

You can view the result of each of the above steps using the [Debug panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#debug)

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#examples "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Be sure to check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Cut Groove in action.

[![cut_groove_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/cut_groove.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/cut_groove.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Cut Operation[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#cut-operation "Permanent link")

*   **Groove:** Cut a V-shape channel into geometry where cutters intersect. This should keep meshes manifold.
*   **Gap:** Cut a gap of even width where cutters intersect. This will create non-manifold geometry.

[![operation_gif](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/cut_groove/cut_groove_2.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/cut_groove/cut_groove_2.gif)

### Cutter Objects[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#cutter-objects "Permanent link")

This section specifies which cutters to use.

*   **Objects/Collection:** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects.
*   **Object Count:** How many cutter mesh objects to use (when in **_Objects_** mode).
*   **Cutter Outset:** Expands the cutter mesh with options for [even thickness](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness)
    .

### Cutter Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#cutter-geometry "Permanent link")

Control how the groove cutter is generated. This can be previewed using the "Final Cutters" [debug view](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#debug)
.

*   **Width:** Width of the cut.
*   **Groove/Cutter Depth:** How deep to make the cutter.
*   **Cutter Boundary Weld:** Distance at which to weld points along the cutter boundary. This is useful for avoiding bad shading and mesh errors along the cut boundary.
*   **Edge Slide Angle:** Slide cutter edges along base mesh edges if they are within this angle.

### Intersection Curve[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#intersection-curve "Permanent link")

Control over the curve generated at cutter intersection. This can be previewed using the "Intersection Curve" [debug view](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#debug)
.

*   **Line Solver:** Which Boolean solver to use to generate the curve.
*   **Simplify Curve:** Remove inline points from curve.
*   **Distance Weld:** Weld points closer than this threshold.

### Boolean[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#boolean "Permanent link")

Settings for final Boolean where groove mesh is cut into the object's surface.

*   **Non-Manifold Solver:** Which Boolean solver to use when one or more inputs is not manifold.
*   **Merge By Distance:** Merge Vertices by distance after Boolean operation.

### Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#normals "Permanent link")

How to calculate normals.

*   **Transfer Normals:** Transfer normals with the Boolean operation. It is recommended to keep this on to avoid shading artifacts.
*   **Match Boundary Normals:** Match the normals on the edge of the groove. This makes the sharp edge appear rounded. [![normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/cut_groove/boundary_normals.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/cut_groove/boundary_normals.gif)
    
*   **Groove Sharp Angle:** Angle at which to mark edges as sharp on the groove faces.

### Groove UVs[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#groove-uvs "Permanent link")

For "Groove" operation only.

*   **Method:** How to generate UVs:
    *   **Inherit Surface UVs:** Project UVs from original surface.
    *   **UV Strip:** Generate new UVs in a strip along the groove.
*   **UV Map:** UV Map to write to.

### Debug[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#debug "Permanent link")

This section lets you inspect the [steps](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/#how-it-works)
 of the process if the modifier isn't behaving as expected.

*   **View.** Outputs geometry from different points in the node graph.
    
    Warning
    
    This changes the output of the graph, make sure to turn off when you're done inspecting.
    
    *   **Intersection Curve:** Output the curve generated by the Boolean intersection.
    *   **Ribbon:** Output the extruded curve or "ribbon".
    *   **Final Cutters:** Output the 'groove cutter' extruded from the 'ribbon'.

---

# Boolean Trim - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#boolean-trim)

Boolean Trim[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#boolean-trim "Permanent link")

=======================================================================================================================

[![Boolean Trim Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/boolean_trim.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/boolean_trim.png)

Cut and 'Trim' a mesh using single-sided cutter object(s).

*   [![Boolean Trim GIF](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_trim.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_trim.gif)
     Trim plane(s) can turn corners
*   [![Boolean Trim Multiple GIF](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_trim_multiple.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_trim_multiple.gif)
     Multiple trim planes can be used with one modifier

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#requirements "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Trim plane(s) must be single sided.
*   Trim plane(s) must extend through the entire mesh.

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#examples "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Please check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Boolean Trim in action.

[![bool_trim_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/boolean_extrude_trim.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/boolean_extrude_trim.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Trim Planes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#trim-planes "Permanent link")

*   **Objects/Collection:** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects.
*   **Object Count:** How many mesh objects to insert (when in **_Objects_** mode).

### Trim[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#trim "Permanent link")

*   **Reverse Trim Direction.** Trim faces on the opposite side of the trim plane(s).
*   **Solver.** The Boolean solver to use for the trim operation.
*   **Un-Sliced Islands.** What to do with mesh islands that aren't sliced by the Boolean operation:
    *   **Trim Per Island.** Delete un-sliced mesh islands based on the direction of the nearest cutting plane(s).
    *   **Keep All.** Keep all un-sliced mesh islands.
    *   **Remove All.** Remove all un-sliced mesh islands.
*   **Offset.** Offset the trim plane(s) with even offset.
*   **Result.**
    *   **Solid.** Output solid mesh if possible. Trimmed section is filled.
    *   **Surface.** Output single-sided mesh. Trimmed section is removed.

### Advanced[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#advanced "Permanent link")

*   **Plane Extrusion.** How much the trim plane is extruded before the Boolean operation.

How it Works[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/#how-it-works "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Trim planes are extruded into solid shapes by a tiny amount in the opposite direction to the trim operation.
2.  These shapes are used for a difference Boolean on the main mesh.
3.  Mesh islands on the trimmed side are deleted.

---

# Offset Surface - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/offset_surface/#offset-surface)

Offset Surface[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/offset_surface/#offset-surface "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================================================

[![offset_surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/offset_surface.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/offset_surface.png)

Offset the surface of a mesh in or out with options for even thickness.

Re-using cutters

This can be useful when re-using the same mesh object to perform multiple cuts/operations.

Here we're using the same basic cutter to perform two cuts:

*   The base mesh has a bevel and is used for a [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
     modifier.
*   A linked duplicate is then using Offset Surface before the bevel then a [Solidify Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/)
     and is used for a [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
     modifier.

[![offset_cutters](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/offset_cut.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/offset_cut.gif)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/offset_surface/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Distance:** Distance to offset surface.
*   **Even thickness:** Method for [even thickness](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness)
    .
*   **Direction:** Direction of offset:
    *   **Normal:** Normal stored on geometry. Will use custom normals if they exist.
    *   **True Normal:** Geometric normal (ignores custom normals).
    *   **Custom:** Custom direction (Specify).

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/offset_surface/#selection "Permanent link")

See [selection settings](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
.

---

# Project to Surface - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#project-to-surface)

Project to Surface[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#project-to-surface "Permanent link")

=========================================================================================================================================

[![Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/surface_blend_9.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/surface_blend_9.png)

Project and blend geometry onto other mesh surfaces. Transfer normals and UVs for a seamless blend.

Similar to [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 but this modifier is added to the "decal/patch" mesh, not the main surface. It's the faster option if you don't need to weld the meshes together.

*   [![Surface Project 1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_1.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_1.gif)
      
    Blend a mesh object to other surfaces. Multiple other mesh surfaces can be specified.
    
*   [![Surface Project 2](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_2.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_2.gif)
      
    Project mesh as a "Decal" onto other objects. This flattens the geometry but keeps the original normals.
    

Open Edges

This modifier works best on meshes with "open edges", i.e. non-solid objects. These edges are used as the base for most [masking methods](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
.

[![open_edges](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/open_edges.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/open_edges.png)

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#examples "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Be sure to check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Project to Surface in action.

[![project_to_surface_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Projection Targets[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#projection-targets "Permanent link")

Choose which meshes to project to.

*   **Target Objects:** How many mesh objects to project to.
*   **Objects:** Choose individual mesh objects to project to.
*   **Collection:** Choose a collection containing mesh objects to project to.

### Projection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#projection "Permanent link")

Define how mesh is projected to surfaces.

*   **Method:** Method for vertex projection: [![Method GIF](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/projection_methods.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_project/projection_methods.gif)
    *   **Wrap to Surface:** Wrap mesh to target surface. Points will be projected to target surface and re-displaced based on their calculated height.
    *   **Blend with Surface:** Project points to target surface then blend between original and projected positions.
    *   **Project Decal:** Flatten geometry to target surface while keeping original normals. Ideal for "floaters" intended for baking.
*   **Surface Offset:** Move projected vertices a fixed amount towards or away from the target surface.

#### Projection Direction[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#projection-direction "Permanent link")

Control the direction and properties of surface projection.

*   **Project Direction:** Define direction of projection rays:
    *   **Object Direction:** Direction in object space (default -Z).
    *   **World Direction:** Direction in world space (default -Z).
    *   **Mesh Normal:** Use individual point normals.
    *   **Mesh Island Normal:** Use the average normal of each mesh island.
    *   **Mesh Tangent:** Use individual point tangents.
    *   **Surface Normal:** Use the normal of the closest part of target surface.
*   **Base Height:** How height is calculated for **_Wrap to Surface_** and **_Project Decal_** projection methods:
    *   **Boundary Center:** Calculate height from a plane defined by the center of the boundary and the projection direction.
    *   **Object Origin:** Calculate height from a plane defined by the objects origin and the projection direction.
*   **Re-Displace:** When using **_Wrap to Surface_**. Direction to apply calculated height:
    *   **Projection:** Apply height displacement using the projection direction.
    *   **Surface Normal:** Apply height displacement using the normal of the target surface.
*   **Ray Length:** Maximum distance rays will collide with target surface. Best to keep at a large enough value that all points collide.
*   **No Hit:** Change behavior of vertices when projection rays miss:
    *   **Unchanged:** Do not move vertex.
    *   **Closest Surface to Max Dist:** Move vertex to the maximum distance in ray direction then snap to closest point on target surface.

#### Projection Masking[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#projection-masking "Permanent link")

Control how projection is masked. See [Mask Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
.

[![falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)

*   **Vertex Group:** Choose a vertex group to project.

### Transfer Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#transfer-normals "Permanent link")

Transfer normals from target surfaces. Internally this uses [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
. Normals are transferred after vertex projection, using the closest target surface.

*   **Normal Domain:** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Normal Masking:** How to mask the normal transfer:
    *   **Projection Mask:** Use the same mask used for vertex [projection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#projection-masking)
        .
    *   **New Mask:** Create a new mask using [mask falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
        .
*   **Vertex Group:** Limit all normal transfer to a vertex group.

### Transfer UVs[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/#transfer-uvs "Permanent link")

Transfer UVs from target surface.

*   **UV Map From:** Which UV Map to transfer from the **surface**.
*   **UV Map To:** Which UV Map to write to on this object.

---

# Solidify Pro - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#solidify-pro)

Solidify Pro[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#solidify-pro "Permanent link")

=======================================================================================================================

[![Solidify Pro Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/solidify_pro_5.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/solidify_pro_5.png)

**Solidify Pro** offers several advantages to Blender's [Solidify Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/solidify.html)
:

1.  Custom normal support.
2.  Edge loops.
3.  Custom profile control.
4.  Inset control.
5.  More creasing control on the rim.
6.  Match Boundary normals.
7.  UV settings.
8.  Front/Back/Rim exclusion/output attributes.

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#examples "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Be sure to check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Solidify Pro in action.

[![solidify_pro_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/solidify_pro.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/solidify_pro.png)

Current Limitations[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#current-limitations "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Not all the settings of Blender's native "Solidify" modifier have been replicated yet. The notable exclusions are:

*   Cannot solidify edges with more than two faces. This is possible with "Complex" mode in the native modifier. [![3faceedge](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/solidify_pro/3_face_edge.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/solidify_pro/3_face_edge.png)
    
*   Float control of **Bevel Weight**. Bevel weight can be set on/off by using the output attribute **_Boundary Edges_**. This attribute can then be specified in a subsequent bevel modifier.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Thickness:** Thickness of solidify operation.
*   **Offset:** Offset the thickness from the center.
*   **Direction:** Direction to extrude:
    *   **Normal:** Normal stored on geometry. Will use custom normals if they exist.
    *   **True Normal:** Geometric normal (ignores custom normals).
    *   **Custom:** Custom direction (Specify).
*   **Even Thickness:** Method for calculating even thickness. See [even thickness settings](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness)
     for more information.

[![even_thickness](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/offset_even.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/offset_even/offset_even.gif)

### Output Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#output-geometry "Permanent link")

In this section you can choose which parts of the solidify operation to keep.

Offset Surface

If you only plan on keeping the front/back of the solidify operation, consider using [Offset Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/offset_surface/)
 as it's much faster.

[![groups](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/solidify_pro/groups.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/solidify_pro/groups.gif)

### Inset[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#inset "Permanent link")

Inset the front or back faces from their boundary edge.

*   **Amount:** Distance to inset faces.
*   **Edge Falloff:** Falloff distance from the edge of the inset. Use to smooth errors at the edge of the inset.
*   **Scale:** Scale faces around their average position. More stable than insetting but can be harder to control.

### Rim Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#rim-geometry "Permanent link")

Control the shape of the geometry created along the rim.

*   **Rim Profile:** Shape of the rim profile:
    *   **Linear:** Flat rim with even edge loops.
    *   **V-Shape:** Specify an angle for a "v-shape" profile.
    *   **Round:** Round profile with even edge loops.
    *   **Custom Curve:** Provide a curve object to apply as a rim profile.
*   **Invert:** Invert the height of the profile.
*   **Reverse:** Reverse the profile curve (swap left/right).

#### Profile Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#profile-geometry "Permanent link")

*   **Profile Height:** How height is calculated along the rim profile:
    *   **Consistent:** Rim profile is consistent height based on the specified thickness.
    *   **Relative:** Rim profile will scale locally based on the distance between points.
    *   **Specify:** Specify a value for profile height.

### Crease[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#crease "Permanent link")

Apply crease values to the rim. - **Front:** Crease value to apply on the front boundary. - **Back:** Crease value to apply on the back boundary. - **Rim Crease:** Method for creasing perpendicular rim edges: - **Edge Angle:** Apply creases by edge angle. - **Signed Edge Angle:** Apply creases by convex/concave angles. - **Vertex Crease:** Use Vertex creaes values for edge creases. - **Rim:** Crease value to apply to selected rim edges.

### Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#normals "Permanent link")

Control normals and sharp edges. - **Mark Sharp:** Mark solidified edges sharp. - **Profile Angle:** Angle along the profile curve to mark sharp (along the rim). - **Rim Angle:** Angle to mark sharp across the rim. - **Extend Sharpness:** Extend sharp edges across the rim. - **Match Boundary Normals:** Match normals along the rim boundary. Requires at least one edge loop.

### Materials[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#materials "Permanent link")

Specify material to use on rim, you can either set material by index or specify a material.

### UVs[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#uvs "Permanent link")

Choose whether and how to generate UVs on rim faces

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/#selection "Permanent link")

Which faces to solidify. See [selection settings](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
 for more information.

---

# Symmetrize - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#symmetrize)

Symmetrize[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#symmetrize "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/symmetrize.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/symmetrize.png)

Mirrors a mesh on a single axis. Similar to Blender's [Mirror Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/mirror.html)
 but with the following improvements:

1.  Correctly mirrors custom normals.
2.  Optional gizmo for displaying the symmetry plane and direction.
3.  Output attribute for mirrored faces.
4.  Global space option without having to use another object.

[![Symmetrize GIF](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/symmetrize/symmetrize.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/symmetrize/symmetrize.gif)

Current Limitations[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#current-limitations "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Limited to a single axis. This can easily be overcome by using another symmetrize modifier.
*   You can't edit the mirrored side when "On Cage" is turned on like with the default mirror modifier.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Symmetry Space:** Coordinate space to use for symmetry.
    *   **Local:** Symmetrize in object's local space (default mirror modifier behavior).
    *   **Global:** Symmetrize in global space based on the world origin.
    *   **Object:** Symmetrize using another objects position and rotation.
*   **Axis:** Which axis to symmetrize on, relative to the **_Symmetry Space_**.
*   **Flip Axis:** Reverse direction of symmetry operation.
*   **Show Axis:** Display location and direction of symmetry operation.
*   **Clipping:** Clip geometry on the mirrored side of the symmetry plane.
    *   **None:** No Clipping. All geometry will be mirrored across the symmetry plane.
    *   **Snap Points:** Points on the mirrored side will be snapped to the symmetry plane.
    *   **Cut Geometry:** Geometry will be sliced on the symmetry plane.

### Merge Points[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#merge-points "Permanent link")

Merge points by distance after symmetry operation.

### UVs[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#uvs "Permanent link")

Options to flip and offset UVs.

*   **UV Map:** Name of UV Map to edit.
*   **Flip U:** Flip mirrored UVs horizontally.
*   **Flip V:** Flip mirrored UVs vertically.
*   **Offset:** Offset mirrored UVs.

### Center Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#center-normals "Permanent link")

*   **Mark Sharp:** Mark center edges as sharp based on angle.
*   **Angle:** Minimum angle for a sharp edge.
*   **Unify Custom Normals:** Face corner normals on center points will average. Sharp edges will be preserved. Only applies if custom normals exist on mesh.
