# Bent Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#bent-normals)

Bent Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#bent-normals "Permanent link")

=========================================================================================================================

[![Bent Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/bent_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/bent_normals_1.png)

Bent normals can improve how surfaces react to ambient lighting, especially in non-path-traced renderers such as game engines.

Usually bent normals are baked to a texture but this modifier will calculate bent normals **directly on geometry**. A cone of rays is cast from each point and the normals are sampled and averaged to find the average direction of ambient light.

*   [![bent off](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bent/bent_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bent/bent_off.png)
      
    **Bent normals off**  
    Unnaturally dark shadows.
    
*   [![bent on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bent/bent_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bent/bent_on.png)
      
    **Bent normals on**  
    Fake bounce light added to shadow areas.
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Factor.** How much bent normals are mixed in with existing normals.

### Collision Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#collision-geometry "Permanent link")

Collide with other objects in the scene.

*   **Object.** Object to collide rays with.
*   **Collection.** Collection to collide rays with.

### RayCast[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#raycast "Permanent link")

Control the distribution of rays used to calculate the bent normals.

*   **Ray Count.** How many rays are cast.
*   **Ray Length.** How long to cast each ray.
*   **Cone Angle.** The angle of the cone that rays are distributed in using a Fibonacci distribution.

---

# Face Weighted Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#face-weighted-normals)

Face Weighted Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#face-weighted-normals "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================================================

[![Face Weighted Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/weighted_normals2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/weighted_normals2.png)

Calculate face weighted normals for a mesh. This is similar to Blender's built-in [Weighted Normals Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/normals/weighted_normal.html)
 but with some differences:

*   Selection Panel lets you limit weighted normals to a specific part of your mesh.
*   Options are designed to be more intuitive and easier to use.

*   [![weighted off](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/weighted/weighted_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/weighted/weighted_off.png)
      
    Un-weighted normals
    
*   [![weighted on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/weighted/weighted_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/weighted/weighted_on.png)
      
    Weighted normals
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Weight.** Derive normal weights from:
    *   **Face Area.** Faces with larger area have more influence over normals.
    *   **Face Strength.** Use the "Face Strength" attribute. [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/meshes/editing/mesh/normals.html#set-face-strength)
        
    *   **Attribute.** Define an attribute to use for normal weighting.
*   **Keep Sharp.** Keep sharp edges on face corners.
*   **Corner Angle Weights.** Include corner angles in weighting. Produces better normals for non -quads.

### Area Weights[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#area-weights "Permanent link")

Have more control over area-weighted normals.

*   **Area Bias.** Bend normals towards faces with larger areas. 1 = Direct weight based on area. Higher values will weight more aggressively to larger areas.
*   **Area Cutoff.** Ignores normals from smaller faces. Cutoff is a percentage of face area compared to the largest connected face.

Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#selection "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options.

---

# Lock Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/lock_normals/#lock-normals)

Lock Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/lock_normals/#lock-normals "Permanent link")

=========================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/lock_normals.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/lock_normals.png)

Creates custom normals for a mesh if none exist. Can also be used to convert between "Free" and "Tangent Space" normals, see the [Blender Docs](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/mesh/write/set_mesh_normal.html#properties)
.

Collapsing Meshes

Tangent Space normals make manual edits to geometry with custom normals much easier. Apply this modifier or use [Add Custom Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/)
 to achieve this.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/lock_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Custom Normal Type:** How to store custom normals:
    
    *   **Free:** Store custom normals as simple vectors in the local space of the mesh. This is efficient and fast to evaluate but does not support deformation.
    *   **Tangent Space:** Store normals in a deformation dependent custom transformation space. This method is slower, but can be better when subsequent operations change the mesh without handling normals specifically.
*   **Normal Domain:** When **_Custom Normal Type_** is set to **_Free_**:
    
    *   **Face Corner.** Store normals on face corners, allows for sharp edges.
    *   **Point.** Store normals on points, cannot have sharp edges.

---

# Repair Bevel Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals)

Repair Bevel Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Bevel Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)

Beveling a mesh with custom normals can lead to unexpected behavior. This tool identifies bevels and replicates Blender's "Harden Normals" functionality found on the bevel modifier.

*   [![default_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)
      
    Bevel with custom normals
*   [![repaired_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)
      
    Repaired Normals

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#examples "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Be sure to check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Repair Bevel Normals in action.

[![normal_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Mesh object with beveled faces you wish to fix
*   (Recommended) Set **Face Strength** to "Affected" or "All" on the bevel modifier
    
    [![Face Strength](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Bevel Detection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection "Permanent link")

How to detect bevelled faces.

*   **Face Strength.** Recommended option. Requires setting face strength on bevel modifier to "Affected" or "All"
    
    *   **Sharp Bevel Edge.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's outer edges.
    *   **Sharp Bevel Corners.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's corners.
*   **Face Area.** Select bevels by their face area.
    
    *   **Max Bevel Area.** Faces with area below this value will be treated as bevels
*   **Attribute.** Use an attribute or vertex group to select beveled faces.
    

### Area Weighting[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#area-weighting "Permanent link")

For weighting normals **inside** the bevel. Available when [Bevel Detection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection)
 is set to **Face Area** or **Attribute**

*   **Area Bias.** Bend normals towards faces with larger areas. 1 = Direct weight based on area. Higher values will weight more aggressively to larger areas.
*   **Area Cutoff.** Ignores normals from smaller faces. Cutoff is a percentage of face area compared to the largest connected face

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options

---

# Repair Boolean Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals)

Repair Boolean Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals "Permanent link")

=======================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Boolean Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)

This tool attempts to fix bad normals caused by booleans. This is intended for destructively applied booleans, for boolean modifiers please consider using [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 as it provides much more accurate and consistent results.

*   [![Bool Normals Default](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)
     Default Boolean Normals
*   [![Bool Normals Repaired](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)
     Repaired Boolean Normals

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Mesh object with destructive booleans applied.
2.  Mesh is un-triangulated i.e. boolean operations have created ngons.
3.  Mesh has sharp edges on boolean seams.

Even if a mesh meets all these requirements it isn't 100% guaranteed to fix all it's normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Error Correct Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#error-correct-distance "Permanent link")

Welds vertices on the proxy mesh that normals are transferred from.

This can fix normal issues but will also introduce more issues as the value increases. There should be a sweet spot where there aren't any issues

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options

How it Works[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#how-it-works "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Splitting the mesh on sharp edges, producing a surface for each "normal island"
2.  Detecting points on the boundary that don't join to any edges inside the island
3.  Re-creating a version of each island without those points
4.  Transferring normals per newly created island back up to the unaltered geometry

---

# Normal Transfer - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#normal-transfer)

Normal Transfer[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#normal-transfer "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================================

[![Normal Transfer Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/normal_transfer_4.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/normal_transfer_4.png)

Transfer Normals from other mesh objects. This can be useful in a variety of situations:

*   [![blend](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/blend.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/blend.gif)
      
    Blend between meshes without affecting geometry.
    
*   [![fix](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/fix.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/fix.gif)
      
    Fix / smooth shading of bad topology. See also [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
     and [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    
*   [![foliage](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/foliage.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/foliage.gif)
      
    Create better shading for foliage cards.
    
*   [![floater](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/floater.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/normal_transfer/floater.gif)
     Match normals of "floater" decals. See also [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#examples "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Examples can be found in the **Normal Modifiers** scene in the [example file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)

[![examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).

### Transfer Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#transfer-geometry "Permanent link")

Choose which meshes to transfer normals from.

*   **Object Count.** How many mesh objects to transfer normals from.
*   **Objects.** Choose individual mesh objects to transfer normals from.
*   **Collection.** Choose a collection containing mesh objects to transfer normals from.

### Transfer[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#transfer "Permanent link")

*   **Method.** How to sample the normal from target surface:
    
    *   **Closest Surface.** Sample normal at closest target surface.
    *   **Project Object Direction.** Raycast-sample target geometry in object space.
    *   **Project Island Normal.** Raycast-sample target geometry along the average mesh island normal.
    *   **Project Source Normals.** Raycast-sample target geometry along mesh normals of this object.
*   **Ray Length (Project Methods).** Maximum length of raycast to target surface.
    
*   **Direction (Project Object Direction Only).** Direction in object space to raycast (default is -Z).
    
*   **Automatic Masking.** Mask falloff for transferring normals, see [Mask Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
    

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#selection "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options.

# Repair Mirrored Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals)

Repair Mirrored Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals "Permanent link")

==========================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Mirrored Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/repair_mirrored.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/repair_mirrored.png)

Mirroring a mesh with custom normals will not mirror the normals. This leads to incorrect normals on the mirrored geometry. This modifier can repair these normals by flipping them in the correct direction.

Symmetrize

Try the [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
 modifier instead for mirroring with custom normals.

*   [![Mirrored Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)
     Unmirrored Custom Normals
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)
     Mirrored Custom Normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#options "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain:** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges)
*   **Method:** Choose which faces get mirrored normals:
    *   **Detect:** Faces and axes will be determined automatically, this should work in most cases.
    *   **[Side of Origin:](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#side-of-origin)
        ** Select and mirror normals based on axis and object origin.
    *   **[Specify:](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#specify)
        ** Specify which faces to flip normals and which axes to flip them on.
*   **Mirror Object:** If using a mirror object, specify it here.

### Side of Origin[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#side-of-origin "Permanent link")

Flip normals for faces on the "mirrored" side. Use this method when using "Bisect" on a mirror modifier or when original geometry doesn't cross the mirror plane(s).

*   **Recalculate Center Normals:** Recalculate Normals for points at the center of specified axes.
*   **Mirror Axis:** Which axes to select and mirror normals on.
*   **Flip Axis:** Flip the direction of the selection of normals to mirror.

### Specify[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#specify "Permanent link")

Choose which faces have flipped normals and what axis to flip them on. Use when geometry is mirrored across both sides of the mirror plane(s) and "Bisect" is turned off.

*   **Mirror Axis:** Which axes to select and mirror normals on.
*   **Selection:** See [Selection Options](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)

---

# Smooth Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals)

Smooth Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals "Permanent link")

===============================================================================================================================

[![Smooth Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/smooth_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/smooth_normals_1.png)

Smooth Normals will smooth mesh normals without smoothing mesh topology. This can be useful in a variety of situations.

*   [![fix_bad_shading](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/fix%20bad_shading.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/fix%20bad_shading.gif)
     Achieve good shading on difficult topology. (Pinched, uneven or distorted normals).
    
*   [![cel_shading](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/cel_contour.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/cel_contour.gif)
     Simplify cel shading contours.
    
*   [![smooth_flatten](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/smooth_flatten.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/smooth_flatten.gif)
     Smooth and/or completely flatten normal islands.
    
*   [![mix_point_normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/mix_point_normals.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/mix_point_normals.gif)
     Mix smoothed point normals for a unique effect (cheap SSS shading).
    

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#examples "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Be sure to check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Smooth Normals in action.

[![smooth_normals_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/smooth_normals.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/smooth_normals.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Keep Sharp.** Keep edges marked as sharp when normals are on face corners.

### Smoothed Proxy Mesh[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smoothed-proxy-mesh "Permanent link")

Generate a smoothed version of the mesh to transfer normals from. The mesh is split on sharp edges before smoothing, you can preview this mesh from the debug panel.

[![smoothed_proxy](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/smoothed_proxy.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/smooth/smoothed_proxy.gif)

*   **Transfer Method.** How to transfer the normals from the smoothed mesh.
    *   **Closest Surface.** Transfer normals from the closest point of the smoothed mesh.
    *   **Point Index.** Transfer normals by their original point index.
*   **Iterations.** How many iterations of smoothing to perform.

### Smooth Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals_1 "Permanent link")

Controls methods of directly smoothing mesh normals.

*   **Smooth Method.** How to smooth normals:
    
    *   **None.** Don't perform any direct normal smoothing.
    *   **Blur Normals.** Fastest Method of smoothing at the cost of being less accurate and not preserving shape as well.
    *   **Smooth Normals.** Iterative smooth function of normals. Slower than blur but can produce better results.
*   **Smooth/Blur Iterations.** How many iterations of smoothing to apply to normals.
    
*   **Face Weight Influence.** Cheap approximation of face weighted normals, lessens the contribution of smaller faces.

### Flatten Normal Islands[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#flatten-normal-islands "Permanent link")

Calculate the average normal direction for entire normal islands (defined by sharp edges).

*   **Amount.** How much to blend to the completely flat island normals.

### Mix Point Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#mix-point-normals "Permanent link")

Mix in smoothed point normals. This can achieve a unique effect, somewhat like sub-surface scattering.

*   **Amount.** How much to blend to the smoothed point normals.
*   **Blur Iterations.** Perform blur iterations on the point normals.

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#selection "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options

# Auto-Flip Faces - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces)

Auto-Flip Faces[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================

[![Auto-Flip Faces Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)

Flipped faces on meshes can cause multiple issues. Traditionally you would check for flipped faces by using the the 'Face Orientation' overlay and resolve areas of flipped faces one by one.

This modifier will detect flipped faces and fix them automatically.

*   [![Faces Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)
     Faces Flipped
*   [![Faces Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)
     Faces Un-flipped

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Invert.** Change direction of entire mesh from outside to inside
*   **Hit Rate.** Tune the detection of flipped faces. Higher values will detect more but increase the risk of false positives.

---

# Change Materials - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/change_materials/#change-materials)

Change Materials[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/change_materials/#change-materials "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/change_materials.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/change_materials.png)

Assign materials or material indices to face selections.

[![Demo](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/change_materials/change_materials_demo.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/change_materials/change_materials_demo.gif)

Up to 6 materials can be assigned per modifier. Selections can be driven by existing material/index or arbitrary vertex/face groups.

Many normalMagic [mesh modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/)
 output attributes that work well with Change Materials.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/change_materials/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Materials to Apply:** The number of materials to apply. This will show a panel for each material with a checkbox to enable/disable.

### Material #[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/change_materials/#material "Permanent link")

The panel for each material assignment, contains two sub-panels: One for selecting faces and one for specifying the material/index.

#### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/change_materials/#selection "Permanent link")

Choose which faces to apply a material to.

*   **Face Group:** Specify a vertex/face group to apply materials to.
*   **Material:** Select faces by a specific material.
*   **Material Index:** Select faces by their material index.
*   **Invert:** Invert selection

#### Material[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/change_materials/#material_1 "Permanent link")

Which material to apply to selected faces.

*   **Set:** Whether to set a specific material or material index.
*   **Material:** Which specific material to apply.
*   **Material Index:** Which material index to apply.

---

# Instance Object - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/instance_object/#instance-object)

Instance Object[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/instance_object/#instance-object "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================

Instances another object (after modifiers) to replace or append to the current objects geometry. This can be particularly useful for re-using details.

[![Instance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/other_tools/instance.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/other_tools/instance.gif)
 Create duplicates of an object without needing to sync all modifier settings to the duplicates.

[![Instance Append](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/other_tools/instance_append.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/other_tools/instance_append.gif)
 Append to and nest modular pieces. Such as these bolt inserts, these can be instanced themselves and used with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 on larger meshes.

Add-on

The normalMagic [Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/)
 has instance operators to make working with this modifier easier:

[![addonpanel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)

*   Create a linked duplicate of an object(s), replace all modifiers with **Instance Object**
*   Select original object being instanced

See [add-on docs](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/instance_objects/)
 for more information.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/instance_object/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Object.** Object to instance
*   **As Instance.** When turned off, will copy object's geometry. Turned on will instance the object [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/instances.html)
    
*   **Replace / Append.** Replace will replace current objects geometry with that of the instance. Append will keep geometry and append the instanced object with additional transform options.

### Transform Instance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/instance_object/#transform-instance "Permanent link")

When appending the instance, these settings allow you to transform the instance relative to the objects origin.

---

# Boolean Extrude - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#boolean-extrude)

Boolean Extrude[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#boolean-extrude "Permanent link")

================================================================================================================================

[![Boolean Extrude Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/boolean_extrude_5.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/boolean_extrude_5.png)

Cuts and extrudes where cutter meshes intersect.

Extrusion Operation[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#extrusion-operation "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   #### **Emboss**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#emboss "Permanent link")
    
    [![emboss](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_extrude_emboss.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_extrude_emboss.gif)
     Slice mesh and extrude intersection faces in or out.
*   #### **Panel**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#panel "Permanent link")
    
    [![panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_extrude_panel.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/boolean_extrude_panel.gif)
     Perform an "Intersect" Boolean and extrude the result into a solid panel.

Equal and Opposite

These two operations are essentially the inverse of each other. Duplicating the object and using opposite operations with different outsets can create interesting effects.

[![Boolean Extrude Combined](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_extrude_combined.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_extrude_combined.png)

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#examples "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Please check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Boolean Extrude in action.

[![bool_trim_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/boolean_extrude_trim.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/boolean_extrude_trim.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#options "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Cutters[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#cutters "Permanent link")

This section specifies which cutters to use.

*   **Objects/Collection:** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects.
*   **Object Count:** How many cutter mesh objects to use (when in **_Objects_** mode).
*   **Cutter Outset:** Expands the cutter mesh with options for [even thickness](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness)
    .

### Extrude[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#extrude "Permanent link")

Control the extrude operation.

*   **Operation:** Choose between Emboss/Panel operations:
    *   **Emboss Surface:** Slice mesh and extrude intersection faces in or out.
    *   **Panel:** Perform an "Intersect" Boolean and extrude the result into a solid panel.
*   **Amount:** Distance to extrude.
*   **Extrusion Method:** How to generate extrusion:
    *   **Extrude Faces:** Extrude intersection area(s) along surface normal. The rim is a strip of quads perpendicular to original surface with options to inset the cut area.
    *   **Offset Boolean:** Offset surface and cut with Boolean cutter(s). The rim is aligned to cutter(s) and there are no options to inset the cut area.
*   **Offset Even:** Use [even thickness](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness)
     settings on extrusion.
*   **Boolean Solver:** Boolean solver to use internally.

### Inset[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#inset "Permanent link")

(**_Extrude Faces_** mode only). Inset the front of the extrude operation.

*   **Scale:** Scale extruded faces around their average position.
*   **Inset:** Inset extruded faces away from the extrusion boundary.
*   **Edge Falloff:** Falloff distance from the edge of the inset. Use to smooth errors at the edge of the inset.

### Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#normals "Permanent link")

*   **Transfer Normals:** Transfer normals from source geometry. Produces the best normals.
*   **Rim Sharp Angle:** Mark edges as sharp on the extrusion rim if they exceed this angle.

### Weld[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/#weld "Permanent link")

Weld points by distance

---

# Boolean Pro - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro)

Boolean Pro[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================

[![Boolean Pro Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)

Boolean Pro is designed to act as a replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier that provides extra functionality. For reference, documentation for Blender's built-in modifier can be found [here](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/booleans.html)
.

Add-on

The [normalMagic Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/)
 includes operators to quickly set up the Boolean Pro modifier, similar to the Bool Tool add-on.

The most powerful feature is transferring the normals automatically from boolean inputs for perfect boolean normals:

[![Perfect Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)

The cutter object can be given an outset (expanded) with even thickness before cutting:

[![Expand Boolean](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_expand.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_expand.gif)

There is also a **_Slice_** operation with optional inset:

| Intersect | Union | Difference | Slice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [![Intersect](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png) | [![Union](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png) | [![Difference](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png) | [![Slice](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif) |

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#examples "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Be sure to check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Boolean Pro in action.

[![boolean_pro_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/booleans.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/booleans.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Cutters[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#cutters "Permanent link")

Specify cutter geometry. You can specify up to 6 objects or use a collection.

### Cutters[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#cutters_1 "Permanent link")

This section specifies which cutters to use.

*   **Objects/Collection:** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects.
*   **Object Count:** How many cutter mesh objects to use (when in **_Objects_** mode).
*   **Outset:** Expands the cutter mesh with options for [even thickness](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#even-thickness)
    .

### Boolean[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean "Permanent link")

*   **Operation:** Which boolean operation to perform.
    *   **Intersect:** Keep the part of the mesh that is shared between inputs.
    *   **Union:** Combine input meshes into a single mesh.
    *   **Difference:** (Default) Subtract input meshes from source mesh.
    *   **Slice:** Slice source mesh with input meshes. This combines "Intersect" and "Difference" into the same output.
*   **Solver:**
    *   **Float:** Simple solver with good performance.
    *   **Exact:** Slower solver with best results on coplanar faces.
    *   **Manifold:** (Default) Fastest solver that only works on manifold meshes, but gives better results.
*   **Inset (Slice Mode):** Offsets the cutter inwards for the intersect boolean to give a gap between meshes.

### Weld[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#weld "Permanent link")

*   **Merge by Distance:** Merge vertices based on distance.
*   **Distance:** Distance at which to merge vertices.

### Shading[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#shading "Permanent link")

*   **Materials:** How to assign materials to new faces.
    *   **Index Based:** Match material index from boolean meshes.
    *   **Specify:** Specify a material to apply to all new faces.
    *   **Transfer:** Transfer materials from boolean meshes. This might not be accurate when using collections.
*   **Transfer Normals:** Use normals from input meshes. Produces the best normals.
*   **Mark Sharp:** Mark all intersection edges between meshes as sharp.

### Solver Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#solver-options "Permanent link")

*   **Result:** Whether to output a solid object or a cut surface.
    *   **Solid:** Output full Boolean result.
    *   **Surface:** Output a cut surface with open edges where new faces would appear.
*   **Sequential (Float solver only):** Perform Booleans one mesh island at a time. This often gives better results but can be slower.
*   **Self Intersection (Exact solver only):** Correctly calculates cases when one or both operands have self-intersections. This involves more calculations making the node slower.
*   **Hole Tolerant:** Optimizes the Boolean output for non-manifold geometry at the cost of increased computational time. Because of the performance impact, this option should only be enabled when the solver demonstrates errors with non-manifold geometry.

---

# Mark Sharp - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp)

Mark Sharp[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================

[![Mark Sharp Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)

Mark Sharp will mark edges as sharp based on edge angle. This is similar to Blender's [Smooth By Angle](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/normals/smooth_by_angle.html)
 modifier but with extra options. There are options to remove and/or add custom normals.

*   [![Smooth By Angle](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)
     **Smooth By Angle**  
    One angle for all edges
*   [![Mark Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)
     **Mark Sharp**  
    Separate angles for convex/concave edges

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Keep Existing:** Keep edges already marked as sharp.
*   **Angle Type:**
    *   **Absolute Angle:** Use a single angle regardless off convexity. This behaves like "Smooth By Angle".
    *   **Convex / Concave:** Specify different angles depending whether edges are convex or concave.
    *   **Edge Creases:** Mark creased edges as sharp.
    *   **Custom Normal Seams:** Detect seams in custom normals and mark as sharp.
*   **Remove Custom Normals:** Will remove any custom normals from geometry and use default normal calculation from sharp edges.

---

# Sharp Edges to Attribute - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edges-to-attribute)

Sharp Edges to Attribute[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edges-to-attribute "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================================================================

[![Sharp Edges to Attribute Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/sharp_to_attribute.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/sharp_to_attribute.png)

Write edge and point attributes based on sharp edges or other arbitrary edge attributes.

*   Edge attributes can be used for:
    
    *   Mark sharp edges as UV seams.
    *   Add a bevel weight to sharp edges.
    *   Detect sharp edges from custom normals and mark them as sharp.
*   Point attributes can be used for:
    
    *   Creating a vertex group for selection in another modifier from sharp edges.
    *   Expanding selection with or without a falloff.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#options "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Sharp Edge Detection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edge-detection "Permanent link")

How to specify edges to write to attribute(s)

*   **Detect From Markup.** Use smooth shading attributes to define sharp edges. This might miss sharp edges defined by custom normals.
*   **Detect Custom.** Detect sharp edges from normals. This will detect sharp edges created from custom normals but can have a small amount of error.
*   **Tolerance (When "Detect Custom" is on).** Change the normal angle difference at which edges will be marked as sharp.

### Expand Points[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#expand-points "Permanent link")

Expand point selection. Can be useful in [Selection Panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#selection)
 of other modifiers.

*   **Point Values.** How selection is expanded:
    *   **Float.** Each expansion gets a lower value creating a falloff.
    *   **Bool.** Each expansion is set to 1.
*   **Expand Iterations.** How many edges to expand point selection.
*   **Falloff (When Point Values are "Float").** Falloff of point selection. similar to [mask falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#base-falloff)
    

### Output Attributes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#output-attributes "Permanent link")

What to name Edge / Point attributes. **Leaving blank will not write to any attributes.**

---

# View Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#view-normals)

View Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#view-normals "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================================

[![View Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/view_normals_eye_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/view_normals_eye_1.png)

View Normals creates geometry to visualize mesh normals on objects.

*   Normals will show up in object mode
*   Normal Colors are currently only available in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
     and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
     viewport shading.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Length.** The object-space length of displayed normals
*   **Normal Domain.** Display normals on:
    *   **Points.** One line will be generated per point.
    *   **Face Corners.** One line will be generated per face corner.
    *   **Faces.** One line will be generated per face.
*   **View.** Specify which normal to visualize:
    *   **Normal.** Display the normal stored on the geometry, custom normals will be included here.
    *   **True Normal.** Display the normal defined by topology, custom normals will be ignored.
    *   **Attribute.** Specify an arbitrary attribute to use.

### Style[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#style "Permanent link")

This section defines how the normal line mesh is generated:

Line [![line](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/normal_line.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/normal_line.png)
 Display normals as a single line. This is the most efficient but can't be colored.

Cylinder / Tapered Cylinder [![line](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/normal_cylinder.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/normal_cylinder.png)
 Display Normals as mesh cylinders with a width and color that will show up in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
 and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
 viewport shading

#### Colors[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#colors "Permanent link")

(When in Cylinder / Tapered Cylinder mode)

*   **Color.** Color to display normals.
*   **Show Default.** Colors normals differently if they match default "Smooth Shading" normals:
    
    [![Show Default](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/normal_default.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/normal_default.png)

# View Sharp - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#view-sharp)

View Sharp[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#view-sharp "Permanent link")

======================================================================================================================

[![View Sharp Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/view_sharp.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/view_sharp.png)

View sharp generates geometry to visualize sharp edges on a mesh. Similar to [View Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/)
 colors are currently only available in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
 and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
 viewport shading.

[![View Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/view_sharp.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/view/view_sharp.gif)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#options "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Detect Sharp.** How to detect sharp edges:
    
    *   **From Markup.** Use smooth shading attributes to define sharp edges. This might miss sharp edges defined by custom normals.
    *   **From Normals.** Detect sharp edges from normals. This will detect sharp edges created from custom normals but can have a small amount of error.
    *   **Both.** Combine both methods above to detect sharp edges.
*   **Tolerance (When "From Normals" is used).** Change the normal angle difference at which edges will be marked as sharp.
    
*   **Width.** Width of pipe geometry created in object-space.
*   **Color.** Color to apply to sharp edge visualization.

# normalMagic 2.0 - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#normalmagic-20)

normalMagic 2.0[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#normalmagic-20 "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================

Welcome to the **normalMagic** documentation. Here you'll find everything there is to know about the tools.

Blender 5.0 Required

normalMagic 2.0 requires **Blender 5.0** or higher. For 4.5 support please use version [1.1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/)

*   [Install normalMagic](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/)
    
*   [Examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
    

Quick Links[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#quick-links "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   [Mesh Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/)
    
*   [Normal Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/)
    
*   [Visualize Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/visualize_tools/)
    
*   [Other Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/)
    

What is normalMagic?[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#what-is-normalmagic "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

normalMagic is a collection of tools for [Blender](https://www.blender.org/)
 that offer powerful new modelling workflows and advanced control over mesh normals.

[![all_tools](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/all_tools_2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/all_tools_2.png)

Controlling normals can be essential for creating high quality 3D artwork. Hard surfaces, organic shapes, foliage, hair and fur can benefit from modifying normals. Sometimes it's the only way to get surfaces shading the way you want.

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**.

Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it is something to be aware of.

See this video for more information: [Custom Normal Issues](https://youtu.be/NoISc01URHg?si=s6NedSzDIFr30zOe)

[Buy on Superhive](https://superhivemarket.com/products/normalmagic)
 [Buy on Gumroad](https://spaghetmenot.gumroad.com/l/normalmagic)

Tool Highlights[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#tool-highlights "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### **Advanced Booleans, perfect normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#advanced-booleans-perfect-normals "Permanent link")

*   Completely avoid boolean normal issues with [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    .
*   Repair normals on previously cut geometry with [Repair Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/)
    .
*   Perform advanced Boolean operations with [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
    , [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
     and [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
    .

### **Seamlessly blend surfaces**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#seamlessly-blend-surfaces "Permanent link")

*   Project positions, normals and UVs with [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
    .
*   Merge, cut and weld projected meshes into a surface with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    .

Convenient auto-masking options means no need for creating and managing vertex groups. Sharp edges can be preserved and transferred.

### **Smooth and Transfer Normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#smooth-and-transfer-normals "Permanent link")

*   Smooth and flatten normals normals to improve shading, achieve good toon lighting or for cheap subsurface scattering effects with [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
    .
*   Transfer and blend normals between unconnected meshes using [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
    .

What's New in 2.0?[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/#whats-new-in-20 "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Changelog - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#changelog)

Changelog[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#changelog "Permanent link")

===================================================================================================

2.0.2[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#202 "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#asset-library "Permanent link")

*   [Solidify Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/)
    :
    *   Fixed bevel weights getting broken on resulting mesh (tooling to control this coming in 2.1).
*   [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
    :
    *   Fixed incorrect normals when custom normals are stored as tangents.

2.0.0[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#200 "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### New Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#new-modifiers "Permanent link")

*   [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
    : Cuts a V-shaped groove or gap where other meshes intersect.
*   [Solidify Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/solidify_pro/)
    : Solidify meshes with advanced profile control. Supports custom normals.
*   [Offset Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/offset_surface/)
    : Offset the surface of a mesh.
*   [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
    : Extrude area of Boolean intersection area in or out.
*   [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
    : Cut and 'trim' a mesh with single-sided cutter objects.

### Existing Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#existing-modifiers "Permanent link")

*   Surface Project renamed to [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
    :
    *   Added Color debug options for projection and normal masks.
*   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    :
    *   Added "Instances" option to insert mesh panel. You can now:
        *   Realize instances and insert using their local transform (if applicable).
        *   Realize instances and insert using their parent insert object's transform (if applicable).
        *   Keep instances, maintain their relative position to the nearest insert geometry.
    *   Debug Color for projection and normal masks.
    *   Surface Boolean is now optional.
    *   Added "Boolean Caps" menu to help with certain edge cases.
*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    :
    *   Has multi-object input like other mesh modifiers.
    *   New "Non-manifold fallback" solver option.
    *   Output attributes for boundary edges and slice faces.
*   [Face Weighted Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/)
    :
    *   Now takes into account corner angle. Triangulated meshes now have better weighting.
*   [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
    :
    *   Added support for mirroring instances.
    *   Added option to convert geometry to an instance before mirroring. This works well when using the object as an insert mesh with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
        .

### Pro Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#pro-add-on "Permanent link")

*   Single file download/install for normalMagic Pro (Asset library is now packaged with the add-on)
*   [Surface Integration](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/surface_integration/)
    . New panel, contains operators for:
    *   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
        
    *   [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
        
*   [Modifier References](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_references/)
    . New panel: show/hide, select/deselect, apply/delete objects referenced by modfiers on the active object.
*   [Object Display](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/object_display/)
    . New panel to manage object visibility.
*   [Boolean Pro Panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/boolean_pro/)
    *   "Advanced Boolean" sub-panel contains new operators:
        *   [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
            
        *   [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
            
        *   [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
            
    *   Modifier Settings now handled by the [modifier defaults](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/)
         system.
*   [Modifier Header Bar](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_header/)
    . Quickly apply modifiers and manage modifier references from the modifier properties panel.
*   [Modifier Defaults](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/add-on/modifier_defaults/)
    . Save default settings for modifiers added via the add-on.
*   Added [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
     and [Mark Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/)
     operators. They are in the modifier header bar by default.
*   All operators now support more than 6 cutters/inserts at a time.

* * *

1.1[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#11 "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#asset-library_1 "Permanent link")

*   [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    : Complete refactor to better match the options on Surface Insert.
    *   Introduced "Wrap" Mode.
    *   Option to turn off normal transfer.
*   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    :
    *   Renamed "Projection Origin" to "Base Height".
    *   "Base Height" no longer shows when in "Blend to Surface" mode.
    *   Fixed Cases where cutters would be flipped when generated causing several bugs.
    *   Refactored height calculation — should be no change to behavior.
    *   Added Debug for projection direction.
    *   Cleaned up all attributes created by modifier.
*   New Modifier: [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
    :
    *   Mirror-like modifier for single axis symmetry.
    *   Works with custom normals.
    *   Local/Global/Object space mirroring.
*   [Repair Mirrored Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
    :
    *   Added Mirror Object option.
*   New Modifier: [Change Materials](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/other_tools/change_materials/)
    :
    *   Assign materials or material indices to face selections.
*   New Modifier: [Lock Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/lock_normals/)
    :
    *   Creates custom normals if none exist.
    *   Allows converting normals to "Tangent Space" which allows for deformation after setting.
*   New Manual Tool: [Add Custom Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/)
    :
    *   Tool version of "Lock Normals" for Object and Edit Mesh modes.
*   [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
    :
    *   Improving sharp edges appearing when transferring normals on small meshes.
*   [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
    :
    *   Fixed broken normals on very small meshes.

### Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/changelog/#add-on "Permanent link")

*   Moved "Link Split Mesh" next to the "Split" Button.
*   Moved "Refresh" button above "Add Asset Library" to encourage use.
*   "Add Asset Library" now runs the "Refresh" operator to avoid adding the same library twice.
*   Fixed error when cutter collection is hidden/disabled.
*   Added "No Change" option to cutter display options.
*   Fixed cutters moving when using the redo panel. This occurred when parenting was on.
*   Now allows example file to live in the same folder as the asset library.

---

# Install - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#install)

Install[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#install "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================

To install normalMagic please see the relevant section for your version:

[**Base Version**](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#base-version-asset-library)
 is installed as an **asset library.**

[**Pro Version**](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#pro-version-add-on)
 is installed as an **add-on** (that is packaged with the asset library).

Base Version (Asset Library)[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#base-version-asset-library "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Base version of normalMagic is installed as an **asset library**, not an add-on.

TLDR

Download the **.zip**, extract to a folder, add the folder as an asset library.

For more detailed instructions see below.

For more information on asset libraries you can check Blender's [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/dev/files/asset_libraries/introduction.html#introduction)

### First Time Install[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#first-time-install "Permanent link")

1.  Download latest .zip file for the closest Blender version (_normalMagic 4.5 v1.0.blend_).
2.  Unzip/extract to a folder in your desired location.
3.  Open Blender and go to **Edit/Preferences/File Paths/Asset Libraries.**
4.  Click + Add Asset Library.  
    [![install_1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_1.png)
    
5.  Navigate to the unzipped folder and press **Add Asset Library**.  
    [![install_2](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_2.png)
    
6.  You should now see "normalMagic" in your asset libraries.  
    **Optional**: Here you can choose how the data is imported by default. Setting this to **Pack** or **Link** will make it easier to update versions. **Link** will also reduce file sizes. See the [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/asset_browser.html#import-settings)
     for more information. [![install_3](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_3.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_3.png)
    

normalMagic should now be installed. Modifiers will now show up under the **Add Modifier** menu:

[![install_check](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/add_modifier.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/add_modifier.gif)

### Update Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#update-asset-library "Permanent link")

To update to a newer version of the normalMagic asset library:

1.  Remove existing asset library under **Edit/Preferences/File Paths/Asset Libraries.**
2.  Repeat [install instructions](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#first-time-install)
     to add new version.

Alternatively you could replace the files in the existing library with the newer ones.

* * *

Pro Version (Add-on)[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#pro-version-add-on "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Pro version of normalMagic is installed as an add-on. The asset library is now packaged with the add-on and can be set up with a single click.

TLDR

*   Download the add-on **.zip** file and drag it into Blender's viewport.
*   Click "Automatic Install" to set up the asset library. (Recommended)

Remove Existing Asset Libraries

Updating from 1.x versions will require removing the existing asset library from **Edit/Preference/File Paths/Asset Libraries**.

### Install Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#install-add-on "Permanent link")

To install/update the add-on simply:

1.  Download the latest "addon" .zip file.
2.  Drag the .zip file into Blender and press OK. [![install_addon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_addon.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/install_addon.png)
      
    You should now see a "Normal Magic" tab in the sidebar of the 3D viewport. [![addon panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/sidebar_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/sidebar_1.png)
    
3.  Press Automatic Install to install the asset library from the add-on\* (recommended). or Open Preferences to add the asset library from elsewhere (See the next section).

\* if you don't see this option make sure to remove old versions of the asset library before repeating the install steps.

### Custom Asset Library Location[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#custom-asset-library-location "Permanent link")

It is advised to install the asset library from the add-on to keep them in sync. However, if you want your asset library imported from another location follow the instructions below.

1.  Remove any existing normalMagic asset libraries from **Edit/Preferences/Filepaths**.
2.  Install the asset library.
    *   Through the Choose Asset Library button in the add-on's Preferences.
    *   Through **Edit/Preferences/Filepaths** (see [Base Version](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#base-version-asset-library)
         instructions).
3.  Refresh Asset library location. (Alternatively you can disable/re-enable the add-on or restart Blender).  
    [![refresh](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/refresh_library.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/refresh_library.png)
    

### Update Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#update-add-on "Permanent link")

1.  Drag the new .zip file into Blender, this will update the existing version.
2.  Press "Automatic Install" to set up the new asset library. If you are using a custom asset library location this will need updating manually.

* * *

Update Existing Scenes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#update-existing-scenes "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Modifiers that already exist in scenes might need updating depending on your asset library setup.

### Linked/Packed Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#linkedpacked-library "Permanent link")

Update modifiers in existing scenes if **library is set to "Link"**:

1.  Go to the outliner and change the view to "Blend File".
2.  Locate the normalMagic file.
3.  Right Click/Relocate.
4.  Select .blend file in the current asset library path.

[![Relocate Library](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/relocate_library.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/relocate_library.png)

### Appended Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/install/#appended-library "Permanent link")

Update modifiers in existing scenes if **library is set to "Append"**:

1.  Add new version of modifier somewhere in your scene.
2.  Go to the outliner and change the view to "Blend File".
3.  Find the modifier under "Node Groups".
4.  Right Click/Remap Users.
5.  Select the newer version (most likely called ".001").

[![Remap Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/remap_modifiers.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/install/remap_modifiers.png)
