# Surface Insert - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert)

Surface Insert[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================================================

[![Surface Insert Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/surface_insert6.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/icons/surface_insert6.png)

Seamlessly join **insert meshes** into the surface of a mesh. Unlike [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
, this modifier is added to the main surface in order to be able to cut and weld the surface geometry.

An **insert mesh** can be thought of as a "decal", "patch" or "plug".

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#examples "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Be sure to check out the [examples file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/examples/)
 to see Project to Surface in action.

[![project_to_surface_examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)

Overview[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#overview "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

There are two primary ways of performing the insert operation:

*   [![Wrap to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)
     **Wrap to Surface**  
    Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)
     **Blend to Surface**  
    Keep world position of geometry and blend to surface via masking. This allows you to keep flat surfaces inside the insert mesh.

The **Surface Insert** Modifier performs the following steps:

1.  Insert meshes are projected to surface geometry using one of the above methods.
2.  Normals, materials and UVs are transferred from the original surface to the insert meshes.
3.  The surface is cut and removed where insert mesh open edges are.
4.  Vertices are matched on both sides of the seam and welded.

Mesh Islands

Insert meshes are performed per mesh island. Only mesh islands with **open edges** will be cut and welded into the original surface.

*   Manifold (solid) mesh islands can be projected but won't affect the underlying surface geometry.
*   Mesh islands that miss a raycast will be skipped entirely and a warning will show on the modifier.

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#requirements "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  A receiving mesh object. This will have the modifier on it.
2.  An insert mesh with open edges. This will be projected, cut and welded into the surface of the receiving mesh.
3.  Insert mesh cannot partially cut into surface, it has to be fully surrounded.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Insert Meshes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-meshes "Permanent link")

This section specifies which meshes to insert.

*   **Objects/Collection:** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects.
*   **Object Count:** How many mesh objects to insert (when in **_Objects_** mode).
*   **Instances:** How to handle instances in insert geometry:
    *   **Realize, Use Transforms:** Realize instances and use their local transforms (if projection direction is "Insert Direction").
    *   **Realize, Ignore Transforms:** Realize instances and use their parent insert object's transform (if projection direction is "Insert Direction").
    *   **Stick to Insert Mesh:** Keep instances and maintain their relative position to the nearest insert geometry.

### Insert Mesh Projection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-mesh-projection "Permanent link")

*   **Method:** How to project the insert meshes to the underlying surface:
    
    *   **Wrap to Surface:** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
    *   **Blend with Surface:** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   **Projection Direction:** Which direction to project insert mesh(es).
    
    *   **Mesh Island Normal:** Use average mesh island normal
    *   **Insert Direction:** Specify a direction in the space of each insert object.
    *   **Object Direction:** Specify a direction in the space of receiving object.
*   **Base Height.** Where to use as the origin of the insert object. This will affect how the objects height is calculated for re-projection.
    
    *   **Boundary Center:** Use average position of mesh boundary.
    *   **Object Origin:** Use insert mesh origin.
*   **Height Offset.** Offsets insert mesh away from surface by a specified height.
    
*   **Ray Length.** Maximum distance an insert mesh can have an effect. Increase
    

#### Projection Masking[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#projection-masking "Permanent link")

This section controls masking the projection of insert meshes to the surface. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**.

[![falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)

Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

#### Insert Mesh Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-mesh-normals "Permanent link")

This section controls how much of the surface normals are transferred to the insert mesh. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**.

*   **Normal Masking**
    
    *   **Projection Mask**. Use the same mask falloff as the projection
    *   **New Mask**. Create a new mask for normals
*   **Recalculate Blend Normals.** Only available when using **Blend with Surface**. This will recalculate the normals on the blended portion of the insert mesh before transferring any from the surface.
    
*   **Match Boundary Normals.** When choosing **_New Mask_** this will make sure the boundary edge of the insert mesh matches the normals on the underlying surface. Turn off to create a hard edge.

If **_New Mask_** is chosen, the settings for the falloff will appear below. Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

### Surface Boolean[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-boolean "Permanent link")

This section controls how the surface is cut for the insert mesh. Turn off if you don't wish to merge surface, this will make the modifier act like [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
. To avoid guess-work it's often best to tweak these settings when viewing the "Boolean Cutters" [debug view](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
.

*   **Weld Surfaces.** Welds vertices between the surface and insert mesh. Turning this off will leave them as separate mesh islands.
*   **Weld Distance.** Distance at which to weld vertices, this takes effect at several points during the process, not just the final weld between surface and insert mesh.
*   **Triangulate Blend Polys:** Triangulate ngons created between the original surface and insert geometry.
*   **Cutting Edges:** Edge group that, if present, will limit which edges will cut into the surface. By default all open edges will cut but you can use an edge group to specify edges.
*   **Boolean Caps:** How to generate boolean end caps. This can allow for complex insert meshes:
    *   **Allow Holes:** Allow insert geometry to have holes. E.g.A circular strip of mesh to make a panel line where the center remains the original surface.
    *   **Allow Tubes:** Insert geometry joins the surface at multiple points. E.g. A handle that joins the surface in two places.
    *   **Flatten Insert Mesh:** Flatten and use insert geometry as the boolean end caps. Can break when there are overhangs but can handle curvature in tight spaces better than the other methods.
*   **Boolean Extrusion.** How far to extrude the cutter shape from the surface. You might need to increase this value if the insert mesh covers a large area of curvature. You can preview cutters in the [Debug](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
     panel. [![Boolean Extrusion](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)
    
*   **Boolean Offset.** Offset the Boolean cutter outside the surface. This only effects the "top" of the cutter and will also help when an insert mesh covers a large area of curvature. You can preview cutters in the [Debug](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
     panel.
*   **Non-Manifold Solver.** Which boolean solver to use when surface mesh is not manifold. Keep this as **_Float_** for most situations.
    *   **Float.** Faster and in a lot of cases more accurate when matching surface points.
    *   **Exact.** Much slower and rarely better. Included "just in case" it helps in rare cases.

### Materials[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#materials "Permanent link")

This section controls how materials and UVs are transferred from the surface to the insert meshes

*   **Transfer Surface Material.** How to transfer materials from the surface to the insert mesh.
    
    *   **None.** Don't transfer any materials.
    *   **Boundary Material.** Transfer the material island(s) that touch open edges.
    *   **All.** Overwrite all insert mesh materials with those on the surface.
*   **Transfer Material UVs.** Transfer UV Maps for the transferred materials.
    
*   **UV Map.** Which UV map to transfer.

### Debug[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug "Permanent link")

This section lets you inspect steps of the process if the modifier isn't behaving as expected.

*   **View.** Outputs geometry from different points in the node graph.
    
    Warning
    
    This changes the output of the graph, make sure to turn off when done inspecting.
    
    *   **None.** Output standard result, no debugging.
    *   **Boolean Cutters.** Output the shapes used to cut the surface.
    *   **Skipped Insert Islands.** Output any mesh islands that did not project due to missed ray casts.

*   [![Boolean Cutters](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)
     Debug Cutters
*   [![Skipped Islands](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/2.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)
     Debug Skipped Insert Islands

# normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#normalmagic-home)

normalMagic Home[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#normalmagic-home "Permanent link")

=======================================================================================================

Welcome to the **normalMagic** documentation. Here you'll find everything there is to know about the tools.

*   [Install normalMagic](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/)
    
*   [Examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/examples/)
    

What is normalMagic?[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#what-is-normalmagic "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

normalMagic is a collection of tools for [Blender](https://www.blender.org/)
 that offer powerful new modelling workflows and advanced control over mesh normals.

[![all_tools](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/all_tools.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/all_tools.png)

Controlling normals can be essential for creating high quality 3D artwork. Hard surfaces, organic shapes, foliage, hair and fur can benefit from modifying normals. Sometimes it's the only way to get surfaces shading the way you want.

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**.

Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it is something to be aware of.

See this video for more information: [Custom Normal Issues](https://youtu.be/NoISc01URHg?si=s6NedSzDIFr30zOe)

[Buy on Superhive](https://spaghetmenot.gumroad.com/l/normalmagic)
 [Buy on Gumroad](https://spaghetmenot.gumroad.com/l/normalmagic)

Tool Highlights[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#tool-highlights "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### **Perfect boolean normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#perfect-boolean-normals "Permanent link")

*   Completely avoid boolean normal issues with [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    .
*   Repair normals on previously cut geometry with [Repair Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/)
    .

### **Seamlessly blend surfaces**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#seamlessly-blend-surfaces "Permanent link")

*   Project positions, normals and UVs with [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    .
*   Merge, cut and weld projected meshes into a surface with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    .

Convenient auto-masking options means no need for creating and managing vertex groups. Sharp edges can be preserved and transferred.

### **Smooth and Transfer Normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#smooth-and-transfer-normals "Permanent link")

*   Smooth and flatten normals normals to improve shading, achieve good toon lighting or for cheap subsurface scattering effects with [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
    .
*   Transfer and blend normals between unconnected meshes using [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
    .

Contact[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/#contact "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

If anything is unclear or missing, please reach out!

[spaghetTools Discord](https://discord.gg/CtpZWGHFhG)
  
[spaghetmenot@gmail.com](mailto:spaghetmenot@gmail.com)
  
[@spaghetmenot.bsky.social](https://bsky.app/profile/spaghetmenot.bsky.social)
  
[@SpaghetMeNot@mastodon.social](https://mastodon.social/@SpaghetMeNot)

# normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/#normalmagic-home)

normalMagic Home[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/#normalmagic-home "Permanent link")

=======================================================================================================

Welcome to the **normalMagic** documentation. Here you'll find everything there is to know about the tools.

*   [Install normalMagic](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/)
    
*   [Examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/examples/)
    

What is normalMagic?[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/#what-is-normalmagic "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

normalMagic is a collection of tools for [Blender](https://www.blender.org/)
 that offer powerful new modelling workflows and advanced control over mesh normals.

[![all_tools](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/all_tools.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/all_tools.png)

Controlling normals can be essential for creating high quality 3D artwork. Hard surfaces, organic shapes, foliage, hair and fur can benefit from modifying normals. Sometimes it's the only way to get surfaces shading the way you want.

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**.

Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it is something to be aware of.

See this video for more information: [Custom Normal Issues](https://youtu.be/NoISc01URHg?si=s6NedSzDIFr30zOe)

[Buy on Superhive](https://superhivemarket.com/products/normalmagic)
 [Buy on Gumroad](https://spaghetmenot.gumroad.com/l/normalmagic)

Tool Highlights[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/#tool-highlights "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### **Perfect boolean normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/#perfect-boolean-normals "Permanent link")

*   Completely avoid boolean normal issues with [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    .
*   Repair normals on previously cut geometry with [Repair Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/)
    .

### **Seamlessly blend surfaces**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/#seamlessly-blend-surfaces "Permanent link")

*   Project positions, normals and UVs with [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    .
*   Merge, cut and weld projected meshes into a surface with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    .

Convenient auto-masking options means no need for creating and managing vertex groups. Sharp edges can be preserved and transferred.

### **Smooth and Transfer Normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/#smooth-and-transfer-normals "Permanent link")

*   Smooth and flatten normals normals to improve shading, achieve good toon lighting or for cheap subsurface scattering effects with [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
    .
*   Transfer and blend normals between unconnected meshes using [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
    .

# normalMagic 2.0 - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#normalmagic-20)

normalMagic 2.0[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#normalmagic-20 "Permanent link")

================================================================================================

Welcome to the **normalMagic** documentation. Here you'll find everything there is to know about the tools.

Blender 5.0 Required

normalMagic 2.0 requires **Blender 5.0** or higher. For 4.5 support please use version [1.1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/)

*   [Install normalMagic](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/install/)
    
*   [Examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/examples/)
    

Quick Links[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#quick-links "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   [Mesh Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/)
    
*   [Normal Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/)
    
*   [Visualize Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/visualize_tools/)
    
*   [Other Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/other_tools/)
    

What is normalMagic?[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#what-is-normalmagic "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

normalMagic is a collection of tools for [Blender](https://www.blender.org/)
 that offer powerful new modelling workflows and advanced control over mesh normals.

[![all_tools](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/all_tools_2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/all_tools_2.png)

Controlling normals can be essential for creating high quality 3D artwork. Hard surfaces, organic shapes, foliage, hair and fur can benefit from modifying normals. Sometimes it's the only way to get surfaces shading the way you want.

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**.

Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it is something to be aware of.

See this video for more information: [Custom Normal Issues](https://youtu.be/NoISc01URHg?si=s6NedSzDIFr30zOe)

[Buy on Superhive](https://superhivemarket.com/products/normalmagic)
 [Buy on Gumroad](https://spaghetmenot.gumroad.com/l/normalmagic)

Tool Highlights[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#tool-highlights "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### **Advanced Booleans, perfect normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#advanced-booleans-perfect-normals "Permanent link")

*   Completely avoid boolean normal issues with [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    .
*   Repair normals on previously cut geometry with [Repair Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/)
    .
*   Perform advanced Boolean operations with [Boolean Extrude](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/boolean_extrude/)
    , [Boolean Trim](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/boolean_trim/)
     and [Cut Groove](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/cut_groove/)
    .

### **Seamlessly blend surfaces**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#seamlessly-blend-surfaces "Permanent link")

*   Project positions, normals and UVs with [Project to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/project_to_surface/)
    .
*   Merge, cut and weld projected meshes into a surface with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    .

Convenient auto-masking options means no need for creating and managing vertex groups. Sharp edges can be preserved and transferred.

### **Smooth and Transfer Normals**[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/#smooth-and-transfer-normals "Permanent link")

*   Smooth and flatten normals normals to improve shading, achieve good toon lighting or for cheap subsurface scattering effects with [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
    .
*   Transfer and blend normals between unconnected meshes using [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
    .

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**. These can be unintuitive to work with if you're unfamiliar with them.

Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it is something to be aware of.

# Common Settings - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#common-settings)

Common Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#common-settings "Permanent link")

=====================================================================================================================

Many tools share common options. They are listed on this page for reference.

* * *

Mask Falloff[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

When two sets of data blend together there are often falloff controls. These can be found across normalMagic in tools such as [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
, [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 and [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
.

### Masking Type[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#masking-type "Permanent link")

#### Boundary Edge Expand[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#boundary-edge-expand "Permanent link")

Select vertices on the boundary edge and expand selection inwards with a linear falloff.

*   **Expand Iterations:** How many iterations to expand mask inwards.

[![Expand Boundary](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/expand_boundary.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/expand_boundary.gif)

#### Boundary Edge Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#boundary-edge-distance "Permanent link")

Select vertices based on their distance to a boundary edge with a linear falloff.

*   **Start Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 1.
*   **End Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 0.

[![Boundary Distance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/boundary_distance.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/boundary_distance.gif)

#### Surface Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#surface-distance "Permanent link")

Select vertices based on their distance to the target geometry.

*   **Start Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 1.
*   **End Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 0.

[![Target Distance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/surface_falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/surface_falloff.gif)

### Base Falloff[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#base-falloff "Permanent link")

The falloff used for values between 0-1. Here it is being used in [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
:

[![Base Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/base_falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/base_falloff.gif)

### Falloff Curve[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#falloff-curve "Permanent link")

Bias the selection towards 1. Here it is being used in [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
:

[![Falloff Curve](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/falloff_curve.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/falloff_curve.gif)

* * *

Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Often we'll want to limit the effects of modifiers to certain areas. The "Selection" section is the same for many tools. It allows for partial selection and, when normals are stored on face corners, hard boundaries.

*   **Vertex Group:** Specify a vertex group to limit modifier's effect.
*   **Face Group (Face Corner Only):** Specify a vertex group that will have hard boundaries.
*   **Material:** Limit the modifier's effect to a specific material.
*   **Invert:** Invert selection.

Here we can see how **Vertex Group** and **Face Group** can be combined:

[![Selection Demo](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/selection.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/selection/selection.gif)

Individual Licenses[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/licensing/#individual-licenses "Permanent link")

For an individual purchasing normalMagic, licenses are as follows:

Team / Studio Licenses[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/licensing/#team-studio-licenses "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

For teams/studios the number of people that use normalMagic is limited by the version purchased. The following team sizes are available:

*   Small Studio: up to 9 users.
*   Large Studio: 10-20 users.

For teams that exceed 20 users, additional licenses should be purchased. This only applies to the asset library's [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
. The licensing is as follows:

*   Asset Library: [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
    .
*   Add-on: [GNU General Public License](https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html)
    .

Early Access Customers

For Studios that bought early access copies: individual licenses can be transferred into a studio license per request. This will discount the studio license by the price paid for each individual license.

Standard Royalty Free License[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Standard Royalty Free license grants you, the purchaser, the ability to make use of the purchased product for personal, educational, or commercial purposes as long as those purposes do not violate the following:

*   You may not resell, redistribute, or repackage the purchased product without explicit permission from the original author.

.

# Mesh Modifiers - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/#mesh-modifiers)

Mesh Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/#mesh-modifiers "Permanent link")

==============================================================================================================

These modifiers affect normals, underlying geometry and other attributes such as UVs.

*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    : Replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier with extra options and accurate normals.
*   [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    : Projects and blends mesh edges onto other mesh surfaces.
*   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    : Projects, cuts, blends and welds other mesh objects into the surface of a mesh.

---

# Boolean Pro - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro)

Boolean Pro[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================

[![Boolean Pro Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)

Boolean Pro is designed to act as a replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier, and provides extra functionality. For reference, documentation for Blender's built-in modifier can be found [here](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/booleans.html)
.

Add-on

The [normalMagic Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/)
 includes operators to quickly set up the Boolean Pro modifier, similar to the Bool Tool add-on.

The most powerful feature is transferring the normals automatically from boolean inputs for perfect boolean normals:

[![Perfect Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)

The cutter object can be given an outset (expanded) with even thickness before cutting:

[![Expand Boolean](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_expand.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_expand.gif)

There is also a **_Slice_** operation with optional inset:

| Intersect | Union | Difference | Slice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [![Intersect](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png) | [![Union](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png) | [![Difference](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png) | [![Slice](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif) |

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Main[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#main "Permanent link")

*   **Operation:** Which boolean operation to perform.
    *   **Intersect:** Keep the part of the mesh that is shared between inputs.
    *   **Union:** Combine input meshes into a single mesh.
    *   **Difference:** (Default) Subtract input meshes from source mesh.
    *   **Slice:** Slice source mesh with input meshes. This combines "Intersect" and "Difference" into the same output.
*   **Object:** Mesh object to use for boolean operation.
*   **Collection:** Collection containing mesh objects to use for boolean operation.
*   **Solver:**
    *   **Float:** Simple solver with good performance.
    *   **Exact:** Slower solver with best results on coplanar faces.
    *   **Manifold:** (Default) Fastest solver that only works on manifold meshes, but gives better results.
*   **Outset:** Expands the cutter mesh with even thickness.
*   **Inset (Slice Mode):** Offsets the cutter inwards for the intersect boolean to give a gap between meshes.

### Weld[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#weld "Permanent link")

*   **Merge by Distance:** Merge vertices based on distance.
*   **Distance:** Distance at which to merge vertices.

### Shading[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#shading "Permanent link")

*   **Materials:** How to assign materials to new faces.
    *   **Index Based:** Match material index from boolean meshes.
    *   **Specify:** Specify a material to apply to all new faces.
    *   **Transfer:** Transfer materials from boolean meshes. This might not be accurate when using collections.
*   **Transfer Normals:** Use normals from input meshes. Produces the best normals.
*   **Mark Sharp:** Mark all intersection edges between meshes as sharp.

### Solver Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#solver-options "Permanent link")

*   **Result:** Whether to output a solid object or a cut surface.
    *   **Solid:** Output full Boolean result.
    *   **Surface:** Output a cut surface with open edges where new faces would appear.
*   **Sequential (Float solver only):** Perform Booleans one mesh island at a time. This often gives better results but can be slower.
*   **Self Intersection (Exact solver only):** Correctly calculates cases when one or both operands have self-intersections. This involves more calculations making the node slower.
*   **Hole Tolerant:** Optimizes the Boolean output for non-manifold geometry at the cost of increased computational time. Because of the performance impact, this option should only be enabled when the solver demonstrates errors with non-manifold geometry.

---

# Surface Project - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#surface-project)

Surface Project[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#surface-project "Permanent link")

================================================================================================================================

[![Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/surface_blend_9.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/surface_blend_9.png)

Project and blend geometry onto other mesh surfaces. Transfer normals and UVs for a seamless blend.

*   [![Surface Project 1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_1.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_1.gif)
      
    Blend a mesh object to other surfaces. Multiple other mesh surfaces can be specified.
    
*   [![Surface Project 2](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_2.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_2.gif)
      
    Project mesh as a "Decal" onto other objects. This flattens the geometry but keeps the original normals.
    

Open Edges

This modifier works best on meshes with "open edges", i.e. non-solid objects. These edges are used as the base for most [masking methods](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
.

[![open_edges](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_project/open_edges.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_project/open_edges.png)

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#examples "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Examples can be found in the **Surface Project/Insert** scene in the [example file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/examples/)
.

[![examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#options "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Transfer Objects[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#transfer-objects "Permanent link")

Choose which meshes to project to.

*   **Object Count:** How many mesh objects to project to.
*   **Objects:** Choose individual mesh objects to project to.
*   **Collection:** Choose a collection containing mesh objects to project to.

### Shrinkwrap[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#shrinkwrap "Permanent link")

Define how mesh is projected to surfaces.

*   **Wrap Method:** Method for vertex projection:
    *   **Nearest Surface:** Move vertices towards the closest point on target surface.
    *   **Project:** Project rays to collide with target surface.
    *   **Project Decal:** Project rays to collide with target surface and flatten geometry while keeping normals. Excludes projection masking options.
*   **Surface Offset:** Move projected vertices a fixed amount towards or away from the target surface.

#### Projection Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#projection-settings "Permanent link")

*   **Projection Direction:** Define direction to project rays:
    
    *   **Object Direction:** Direction in object space (default is -Z).
    *   **World Direction:** Direction in global space (default is -Z).
    *   **Surface Normal:** Surface normal of the closest target surface.
    *   **Mesh Island Normal:** Use the average normal of each mesh island.
    *   **Surface Tangent:** Direction of surface at edge boundaries.
*   **Ray Length:** Maximum distance rays will collide with target surface.
    
*   **No Hit:** Change behavior of vertices when projection rays miss:
    *   **Unchanged:** Do not move vertex.
    *   **Closest Surface to Max Dist:** Move vertex to the maximum distance in ray direction then snap to closest point on target surface.

#### Masking[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#masking "Permanent link")

Control how projection is masked. See [Mask Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
.

[![falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)

*   **Vertex Group:** Choose a vertex group to project.

### Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#normals "Permanent link")

Define how normals are transferred from target surfaces. This uses [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
 internally. Normals are transferred after vertex projection, using the closest target surface.

*   **Normal Domain:** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Use Shrinkwrap Amount:** Use the same mask used for vertex [projection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#masking)
    .
*   **Amount:** Specify a vertex group or single value to choose how much normals are transferred.
*   **Automatic Masking:** Use [mask falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
     settings to make a new mask for transferring normals.

### UVs[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#uvs "Permanent link")

Transfer UVs from target surface.

*   **Transfer UVs:** Turn UV transfer on/off.
*   **UV Map From:** Which UV Map to transfer from the **surface**.
*   **UV Map To:** Which UV Map to write to on this object.

#### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/#selection "Permanent link")

Limit UV transfer to specific faces.

*   **Selection:** Which faces to modify UVs for. Defaults to "on", applying to every face.
*   **Material:** Limit UV modification to a specific material.
*   **Invert Selection:** Inverts the selection to modify UVs on every face that is not selected using the above options.

---

# Normal Modifiers - normalMagic

Normal Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/#normal-modifiers "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================

These modifiers effect the normals of a mesh and nothing else.

*   [Bent Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/)
    
*   [Repair Bevel Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
    
*   [Repair Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/)
