# Normal Transfer - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#normal-transfer)

Normal Transfer[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#normal-transfer "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================================

[![Normal Transfer Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/normal_transfer_4.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/normal_transfer_4.png)

Transfer Normals from other mesh objects. This can be useful in a variety of situations:

*   [![blend](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/blend.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/blend.gif)
      
    Blend between meshes without affecting geometry.
    
*   [![fix](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/fix.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/fix.gif)
      
    Fix / smooth shading of bad topology. See also [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
     and [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    
*   [![foliage](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/foliage.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/foliage.gif)
      
    Create better shading for foliage cards.
    
*   [![floater](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/floater.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/normal_transfer/floater.gif)
     Match normals of "floater" decals. See also [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#examples "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Examples can be found in the **Normal Modifiers** scene in the [example file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/examples/)

[![examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).

### Transfer Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#transfer-geometry "Permanent link")

Choose which meshes to transfer normals from.

*   **Object Count.** How many mesh objects to transfer normals from.
*   **Objects.** Choose individual mesh objects to transfer normals from.
*   **Collection.** Choose a collection containing mesh objects to transfer normals from.

### Transfer[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#transfer "Permanent link")

*   **Method.** How to sample the normal from target surface:
    
    *   **Closest Surface.** Sample normal at closest target surface.
    *   **Project Object Direction.** Raycast-sample target geometry in object space.
    *   **Project Island Normal.** Raycast-sample target geometry along the average mesh island normal.
    *   **Project Source Normals.** Raycast-sample target geometry along mesh normals of this object.
*   **Ray Length (Project Methods).** Maximum length of raycast to target surface.
    
*   **Direction (Project Object Direction Only).** Direction in object space to raycast (default is -Z).
    
*   **Automatic Masking.** Mask falloff for transferring normals, see [Mask Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
    

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#selection "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options.

---

# Smooth Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals)

Smooth Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals "Permanent link")

===============================================================================================================================

[![Smooth Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/smooth_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/smooth_normals_1.png)

Smooth Normals will smooth mesh normals without smoothing mesh topology. This can be useful in a variety of situations.

*   [![fix_bad_shading](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/fix%20bad_shading.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/fix%20bad_shading.gif)
     Achieve good shading on difficult topology. (Pinched, uneven or distorted normals).
    
*   [![cel_shading](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/cel_contour.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/cel_contour.gif)
     Simplify cel shading contours.
    
*   [![smooth_flatten](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/smooth_flatten.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/smooth_flatten.gif)
     Smooth and/or completely flatten normal islands.
    
*   [![mix_point_normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/mix_point_normals.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/mix_point_normals.gif)
     Mix smoothed point normals for a unique effect (cheap SSS shading).
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Keep Sharp.** Keep edges marked as sharp when normals are on face corners.

### Smoothed Proxy Mesh[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smoothed-proxy-mesh "Permanent link")

Generate a smoothed version of the mesh to transfer normals from. The mesh is split on sharp edges before smoothing, you can preview this mesh from the debug panel.

[![smoothed_proxy](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/smoothed_proxy.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/smooth/smoothed_proxy.gif)

*   **Transfer Method.** How to transfer the normals from the smoothed mesh.
    *   **Closest Surface.** Transfer normals from the closest point of the smoothed mesh.
    *   **Point Index.** Transfer normals by their original point index.
*   **Iterations.** How many iterations of smoothing to perform.

### Smooth Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals_1 "Permanent link")

Controls methods of directly smoothing mesh normals.

*   **Smooth Method.** How to smooth normals:
    
    *   **None.** Don't perform any direct normal smoothing.
    *   **Blur Normals.** Fastest Method of smoothing at the cost of being less accurate and not preserving shape as well.
    *   **Smooth Normals.** Iterative smooth function of normals. Slower than blur but can produce better results.
*   **Smooth/Blur Iterations.** How many iterations of smoothing to apply to normals.
    
*   **Face Weight Influence.** Cheap approximation of face weighted normals, lessens the contribution of smaller faces.

### Flatten Normal Islands[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#flatten-normal-islands "Permanent link")

Calculate the average normal direction for entire normal islands (defined by sharp edges).

*   **Amount.** How much to blend to the completely flat island normals.

### Mix Point Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#mix-point-normals "Permanent link")

Mix in smoothed point normals. This can achieve a unique effect, somewhat like sub-surface scattering.

*   **Amount.** How much to blend to the smoothed point normals.
*   **Blur Iterations.** Perform blur iterations on the point normals.

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#selection "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options

---

# Tools Overview - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/tools_overview/#tools-overview)

Tools Overview[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/tools_overview/#tools-overview "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**. These can be unintuitive to work with if you're unfamiliar with them.

Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it is something to be aware of.

Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/tools_overview/#asset-library "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The core of normalMagic is an asset library. You will find links to all the tools in the sidebar under the following categories:

*   [Mesh Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/)
    
*   [Normal Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/)
    
*   [Visualize Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/)
    
*   [Other Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/)
    
*   [Manual Tools](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/manual_tools/)
    

Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/tools_overview/#add-on "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Included with the Pro version, the add-on makes it easier to set up some of the modifiers.

[Add-on Documentation](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/)

---

# Bent Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#bent-normals)

Bent Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#bent-normals "Permanent link")

=========================================================================================================================

[![Bent Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/bent_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/bent_normals_1.png)

Bent normals can improve how surfaces react to ambient lighting, especially in non-path-traced renderers such as game engines.

Usually bent normals are baked to a texture but this modifier will calculate bent normals **directly on geometry**. A cone of rays is cast from each point and the normals are sampled and averaged to find the average direction of ambient light.

*   [![bent off](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bent/bent_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bent/bent_off.png)
      
    **Bent normals off**  
    Unnaturally dark shadows.
    
*   [![bent on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bent/bent_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bent/bent_on.png)
      
    **Bent normals on**  
    Fake bounce light added to shadow areas.
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Factor.** How much bent normals are mixed in with existing normals.

### Collision Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#collision-geometry "Permanent link")

Collide with other objects in the scene.

*   **Object.** Object to collide rays with.
*   **Collection.** Collection to collide rays with.

### RayCast[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/bent_normals/#raycast "Permanent link")

Control the distribution of rays used to calculate the bent normals.

*   **Ray Count.** How many rays are cast.
*   **Ray Length.** How long to cast each ray.
*   **Cone Angle.** The angle of the cone that rays are distributed in using a Fibonacci distribution.

---

# Install - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#install)

Install[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#install "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================

normalMagic is installed as an **asset library** in Blender. There is an optional **add-on** included with the Pro version which makes it easier and faster to set up certain modifiers.

Timestamped install video:

Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#asset-library "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TLDR

Download the **.zip**, extract to a folder, add the folder as an asset library.

For more detailed instructions see below.

For more information on asset libraries you can check Blender's [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/dev/files/asset_libraries/introduction.html#introduction)

### First Time Install[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#first-time-install "Permanent link")

1.  Download latest .zip file for the closest Blender version (_normalMagic 4.5 v1.0.blend_).
2.  Unzip/extract to a folder in your desired location.
3.  Open Blender and go to **Edit/Preferences/File Paths/Asset Libraries.**
4.  Click + Add Asset Library. [![install_1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_1.png)
    
5.  Navigate to the unzipped folder and press **Add Asset Library**. [![install_2](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_2.png)
    
6.  You should now see "normalMagic" in your asset libraries.  
    **Optional**: Here you can choose how the data is imported by default. Setting this to 'Link' will reduce file sizes and make it easier to update versions. See the [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/asset_browser.html#import-settings)
     for more information. [![install_3](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_3.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_3.png)
    

normalMagic should now be installed. Modifiers will now show up under the **Add Modifier** menu. [![install_check](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_check.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_check.png)

### Update Version[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#update-version "Permanent link")

To update to a newer version of normalMagic:

1.  Remove existing asset library under **Edit/Preferences/File Paths/Asset Libraries.**
2.  Repeat [install instructions](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#first-time-install)
     to add new version.

### Update Existing Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#update-existing-modifiers "Permanent link")

#### Linked Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#linked-library "Permanent link")

Update modifiers in existing scenes if **library is set to "Link"**:

1.  Go to the outliner and change the view to "Blend File".
2.  Locate the normalMagic file.
3.  Right Click/Relocate.
4.  Select .blend file in the current asset library path.

[![Relocate Library](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/relocate_library.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/relocate_library.png)

#### Appended Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#appended-library "Permanent link")

Update modifiers in existing scenes if **library is set to "Append"**:

1.  Add new version of modifier somewhere in your scene.
2.  Go to the outliner and change the view to "Blend File".
3.  Find the modifier under "Node Groups".
4.  Right Click/Remap Users.
5.  Select the newer version (most likely called ".001").

[![Remap Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/remap_modifiers.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/remap_modifiers.png)

Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#add-on "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Pro version

The normalMagic add-on is available to the **Pro** tier only.

[Add-on Documentation](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/)

To install/update the add-on simply:

1.  Download the latest "addon" .zip file.
2.  Drag the .zip file into Blender and press OK. [![install_addon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_addon.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/install/install_addon.png)
    

You should now see a "Normal Magic" tab in the sidebar of the 3D viewport.

[![addon panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/addon_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/addon_panel.png)

---

# Face Weighted Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#face-weighted-normals)

Face Weighted Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#face-weighted-normals "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================================================

[![Face Weighted Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/weighted_normals2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/weighted_normals2.png)

Calculate face weighted normals for a mesh. This is similar to Blender's built-in [Weighted Normals Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/normals/weighted_normal.html)
 but with some differences:

*   Selection Panel lets you limit weighted normals to a specific part of your mesh.
*   Options are designed to be more intuitive.

"Corner Angle" weighting is not yet implemented

This can create some odd shading on certain topology and is planned for a future update.

*   [![weighted off](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/weighted/weighted_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/weighted/weighted_off.png)
      
    Un-weighted normals
    
*   [![weighted on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/weighted/weighted_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/weighted/weighted_on.png)
      
    Weighted normals
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Weight.** Derive normal weights from:
    *   **Face Area.** Faces with larger area have more influence over normals.
    *   **Face Strength.** Use the "Face Strength" attribute. [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/meshes/editing/mesh/normals.html#set-face-strength)
        
    *   **Attribute.** Define an attribute to use for normal weighting.
*   **Keep Sharp.** Keep sharp edges on face corners.

### Area Weights[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#area-weights "Permanent link")

Have more control over area-weighted normals.

*   **Area Bias.** Bend normals towards faces with larger areas. 1 = Direct weight based on area. Higher values will weight more aggressively to larger areas.
*   **Area Cutoff.** Ignores normals from smaller faces. Cutoff is a percentage of face area compared to the largest connected face.

Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#selection "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options.

---

# Common Settings - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#common-settings)

Common Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#common-settings "Permanent link")

=====================================================================================================================

Many tools share common options. They are listed on this page for reference.

* * *

Mask Falloff[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#mask-falloff "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

When two sets of data blend together there are often falloff controls. These can be found across normalMagic in tools such as [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
, [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 and [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
.

### Masking Type[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#masking-type "Permanent link")

#### Boundary Edge Expand[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#boundary-edge-expand "Permanent link")

Select vertices on the boundary edge and expand selection inwards with a linear falloff.

*   **Expand Iterations:** How many iterations to expand mask inwards.

[![Expand Boundary](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/expand_boundary.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/expand_boundary.gif)

#### Boundary Edge Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#boundary-edge-distance "Permanent link")

Select vertices based on their distance to a boundary edge with a linear falloff.

*   **Start Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 1.
*   **End Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 0.

[![Boundary Distance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/boundary_distance.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/boundary_distance.gif)

#### Surface Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#surface-distance "Permanent link")

Select vertices based on their distance to the target geometry.

*   **Start Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 1.
*   **End Distance:** Distance at which vertices will get a value of 0.

[![Target Distance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/surface_falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/surface_falloff.gif)

### Base Falloff[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#base-falloff "Permanent link")

The falloff used for values between 0-1. Here it is being used in [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
:

[![Base Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/base_falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/base_falloff.gif)

### Falloff Curve[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#falloff-curve "Permanent link")

Bias the selection towards 1. Here it is being used in [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
:

[![Falloff Curve](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/falloff_curve.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/falloff_curve.gif)

* * *

Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Often we'll want to limit the effects of modifiers to certain areas. The "Selection" section is the same for many tools. It allows for partial selection and, when normals are stored on face corners, hard boundaries.

*   **Vertex Group:** Specify a vertex group to limit modifier's effect.
*   **Face Group (Face Corner Only):** Specify a vertex group that will have hard boundaries.
*   **Material:** Limit the modifier's effect to a specific material.
*   **Invert:** Invert selection.

Here we can see how **Vertex Group** and **Face Group** can be combined:

[![Selection Demo](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/selection.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/selection/selection.gif)

---

# Surface Insert - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert)

Surface Insert[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================================================

[![Surface Insert Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/surface_insert6.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/surface_insert6.png)

Seamlessly join **insert meshes** into the surface of a mesh.

An **insert mesh** can be thought of as a "decal", "patch" or "plug".

There are two primary ways of performing the insert operation:

*   [![Wrap to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)
     **Wrap to Surface**  
    Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)
     **Blend to Surface**  
    Keep world position of geometry and blend to surface via masking. This allows you to keep flat surfaces inside the insert mesh.

The **Surface Insert** Modifier performs the following steps:

1.  Insert meshes are projected to surface geometry using one of the above methods.
2.  Normals, materials and UVs are transferred from the original surface to the insert meshes.
3.  The surface is cut and removed where insert mesh open edges are.
4.  Vertices are matched on both sides of the seam and welded.

Mesh Islands

Insert meshes are performed per mesh island. Only mesh islands with **open edges** will be cut and welded into the original surface.

*   Manifold (solid) mesh islands can be projected but won't affect the underlying surface geometry.
*   Mesh islands that miss a raycast will be skipped entirely and a warning will show on the modifier.

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#requirements "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  A receiving mesh object. This will have the modifier on it.
2.  An insert mesh with open edges. This will be projected, cut and welded into the surface of the receiving mesh.
3.  Insert mesh cannot partially cut into surface, it has to be fully surrounded.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Insert Meshes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-meshes "Permanent link")

This section specifies which meshes to insert.

*   **Objects/Collection:** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects.
*   **Object Count:** How many mesh objects to insert (when in **_Objects_** mode).

### Insert Mesh Projection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-mesh-projection "Permanent link")

*   **Method:** How to project the insert meshes to the underlying surface:
    
    *   **Wrap to Surface:** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
    *   **Blend with Surface:** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   **Projection Direction:** Which direction to project insert mesh(es).
    
    *   **Mesh Island Normal:** Use average mesh island normal
    *   **Insert Direction:** Specify a direction in the space of each insert object.
    *   **Object Direction:** Specify a direction in the space of receiving object.
*   **Projection Origin.** Where to use as the origin of the insert object. This will affect how the objects height is calculated for re-projection.
    
*   **Height Offset.** Offsets insert mesh away from surface by a specified height.
    
*   **Ray Length.** Maximum distance an insert mesh can have an effect.
    

#### Projection Masking[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#projection-masking "Permanent link")

This section controls masking the projection of insert meshes to the surface. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**.

[![falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)

Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

#### Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#normals "Permanent link")

This section controls how much of the surface normals are transferred to the insert mesh. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**.

*   **Normal Masking**
    
    *   **Projection Mask**. Use the same mask falloff as the projection
    *   **New Mask**. Create a new mask for normals
*   **Recalculate Blend Normals.** Only available when using **Blend with Surface**. This will recalculate the normals on the blended portion of the insert mesh before transferring any from the surface.
    
*   **Match Boundary Normals.** When choosing **_New Mask_** this will make sure the boundary edge of the insert mesh matches the normals on the underlying surface. Turn off to create a hard edge.

If **_New Mask_** is chosen, the settings for the falloff will appear below. Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

### Surface Boolean[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-boolean "Permanent link")

This section controls how the surface is cut for the insert mesh.

*   **Weld Surface.** Welds vertices between the surface and insert mesh. Turning this off will leave them as separate mesh islands.
*   **Weld Distance.** Distance at which to weld vertices, this takes effect at several points during the process, not just the final weld between surface and insert mesh.
*   **Boolean Extrusion.** How far to extrude the cutter shape from the surface. You might need to increase this value if the insert mesh covers a large area of curvature. You can preview cutters in the [Debug](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
     panel. [![Boolean Extrusion](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)
    
*   **Boolean Offset.** Offset the Boolean cutter outside the surface. This only effects the "top" of the cutter and will also help when an insert mesh covers a large area of curvature. You can preview cutters in the [Debug](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
     panel.
*   **Non-Manifold Solver.** Which boolean solver to use when surface mesh is not manifold. Keep this as **_Float_** for most situations.
    *   **Float.** Faster and in a lot of cases more accurate when matching surface points.
    *   **Exact.** Much slower and rarely better. Included "just in case" it helps in rare cases.

### Materials[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#materials "Permanent link")

This section controls how materials and UVs are transferred from the surface to the insert meshes

*   **Transfer Surface Material.** How to transfer materials from the surface to the insert mesh.
    
    *   **None.** Don't transfer any materials.
    *   **Boundary Material.** Transfer the material island(s) that touch open edges.
    *   **All.** Overwrite all insert mesh materials with those on the surface.
*   **Transfer Material UVs.** Transfer UV Maps for the transferred materials.
    
*   **UV Map.** Which UV map to transfer.

### Debug[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug "Permanent link")

This section lets you inspect certain points along the process if the modifier isn't behaving as expected.

*   **View.** Outputs geometry from different points in the node graph.
    
    Warning
    
    This changes the output of the graph, make sure to turn off when done inspecting.
    
    *   **None.** Output standard result, no debugging.
    *   **Boolean Cutters.** Output the shapes used to cut the surface.
    *   **Skipped Insert Islands.** Output any mesh islands that did not project due to missed ray casts.

*   [![Boolean Cutters](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)
     Debug Cutters
*   [![Skipped Islands](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)
     Debug Skipped Insert Islands

---

# Install - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#install)

Install[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#install "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================

normalMagic is installed as an **asset library** in Blender. There is an optional **add-on** included with the Pro version which makes it easier and faster to set up certain modifiers.

Timestamped install video:

Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#asset-library "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

TLDR

Download the **.zip**, extract to a folder, add the folder as an asset library.

For more detailed instructions see below.

For more information on asset libraries you can check Blender's [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/dev/files/asset_libraries/introduction.html#introduction)

### First Time Install[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#first-time-install "Permanent link")

1.  Download latest .zip file for the closest Blender version (_normalMagic 4.5 v1.0.blend_).
2.  Unzip/extract to a folder in your desired location.
3.  Open Blender and go to **Edit/Preferences/File Paths/Asset Libraries.**
4.  Click + Add Asset Library. [![install_1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_1.png)
    
5.  Navigate to the unzipped folder and press **Add Asset Library**. [![install_2](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_2.png)
    
6.  You should now see "normalMagic" in your asset libraries.  
    **Optional**: Here you can choose how the data is imported by default. Setting this to 'Link' will reduce file sizes and make it easier to update versions. See the [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/asset_browser.html#import-settings)
     for more information. [![install_3](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_3.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_3.png)
    

normalMagic should now be installed. Modifiers will now show up under the **Add Modifier** menu. [![install_check](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_check.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_check.png)

### Update Version[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#update-version "Permanent link")

To update to a newer version of normalMagic:

1.  Remove existing asset library under **Edit/Preferences/File Paths/Asset Libraries.**
2.  Repeat [install instructions](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#first-time-install)
     to add new version.

### Update Existing Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#update-existing-modifiers "Permanent link")

#### Linked Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#linked-library "Permanent link")

Update modifiers in existing scenes if **library is set to "Link"**:

1.  Go to the outliner and change the view to "Blend File".
2.  Locate the normalMagic file.
3.  Right Click/Relocate.
4.  Select .blend file in the current asset library path.

[![Relocate Library](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/relocate_library.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/relocate_library.png)

#### Appended Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#appended-library "Permanent link")

Update modifiers in existing scenes if **library is set to "Append"**:

1.  Add new version of modifier somewhere in your scene.
2.  Go to the outliner and change the view to "Blend File".
3.  Find the modifier under "Node Groups".
4.  Right Click/Remap Users.
5.  Select the newer version (most likely called ".001").

[![Remap Modifiers](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/remap_modifiers.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/remap_modifiers.png)

Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#add-on "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Pro version

The normalMagic add-on is available to the **Pro** tier only.

[Add-on Documentation](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/)

To install/update the add-on simply:

1.  Download the latest "addon" .zip file.
2.  Drag the .zip file into Blender and press OK. [![install_addon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_addon.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/install/install_addon.png)
    

You should now see a "Normal Magic" tab in the sidebar of the 3D viewport.

[![addon panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/addon_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/addon_panel.png)

---

# Repair Bevel Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals)

Repair Bevel Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Bevel Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)

Beveling a mesh with custom normals can lead to unexpected behavior. This tool identifies bevels and replicates Blender's "Harden Normals" functionality found on the bevel modifier.

*   [![default_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)
      
    Bevel with custom normals
*   [![repaired_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)
      
    Repaired Normals

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Mesh object with beveled faces you wish to fix
*   (Recommended) Set **Face Strength** to "Affected" or "All" on the bevel modifier
    
    [![Face Strength](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Bevel Detection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection "Permanent link")

How to detect bevelled faces.

*   **Face Strength.** Recommended option. Requires setting face strength on bevel modifier to "Affected" or "All"
    
    *   **Sharp Bevel Edge.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's outer edges.
    *   **Sharp Bevel Corners.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's corners.
*   **Face Area.** Select bevels by their face area.
    
    *   **Max Bevel Area.** Faces with area below this value will be treated as bevels
*   **Attribute.** Use an attribute or vertex group to select beveled faces.
    

### Area Weighting[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#area-weighting "Permanent link")

For weighting normals **inside** the bevel. Available when [Bevel Detection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection)
 is set to **Face Area** or **Attribute**

*   **Area Bias.** Bend normals towards faces with larger areas. 1 = Direct weight based on area. Higher values will weight more aggressively to larger areas.
*   **Area Cutoff.** Ignores normals from smaller faces. Cutoff is a percentage of face area compared to the largest connected face

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options

---

# Repair Boolean Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals)

Repair Boolean Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals "Permanent link")

=======================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Boolean Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)

This tool attempts to fix bad normals caused by booleans. This is intended for destructively applied booleans, for boolean modifiers please consider using [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 as it provides much more accurate and consistent results.

*   [![Bool Normals Default](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)
     Default Boolean Normals
*   [![Bool Normals Repaired](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)
     Repaired Boolean Normals

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Mesh object with destructive booleans applied.
2.  Mesh is un-triangulated i.e. boolean operations have created ngons.
3.  Mesh has sharp edges on boolean seams.

Even if a mesh meets all these requirements it isn't 100% guaranteed to fix all it's normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Error Correct Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#error-correct-distance "Permanent link")

Welds vertices on the proxy mesh that normals are transferred from.

This can fix normal issues but will also introduce more issues as the value increases. There should be a sweet spot where there aren't any issues

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection)
 options

How it Works[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#how-it-works "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Splitting the mesh on sharp edges, producing a surface for each "normal island"
2.  Detecting points on the boundary that don't join to any edges inside the island
3.  Re-creating a version of each island without those points
4.  Transferring normals per newly created island back up to the unaltered geometry

---

# Repair Mirrored Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals)

Repair Mirrored Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals "Permanent link")

==========================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Mirrored Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/repair_mirrored.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/repair_mirrored.png)

Mirroring a mesh with custom normals will not mirror the normals. This leads to incorrect normals on the mirrored geometry.

This modifier will detect mesh islands with flipped normals and what axis they need flipping on.

*   [![Mirrored Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)
     Unmirrored Custom Normals
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)
     Mirrored Custom Normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#options "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges)
*   **Method.** Choose which faces get mirrored normals:
    *   **Detect.** Faces and axes will be determined automatically, this should work in most cases.
    *   **Side of Origin.** Select and mirror normals based on axis and object origin.
    *   **Specify.** Specify which faces to flip normals and which axes to flip them on.

### Side of Origin[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#side-of-origin "Permanent link")

When using this method, other options become available:

*   **Recalculate Center Normals.** Recalculate Normals for points at the center of specified axes
*   **Mirror Axis.** Which axes to select and mirror normals on.
*   **Flip Axis.** Flip the direction of the selection of normals to mirror.

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#selection "Permanent link")

See [Selection Options](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection)

# Changelog - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/changelog/#changelog)

Changelog[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/changelog/#changelog "Permanent link")

===================================================================================================

1.1.2[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/changelog/#112 "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/changelog/#add-on "Permanent link")

*   Fix For Blender 5.0 moving the "Smooth by Angle" modifier

1.1[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/changelog/#11 "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Asset Library[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/changelog/#asset-library "Permanent link")

*   [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    : Complete refactor to better match the options on Surface Insert.
    *   Introduced "Wrap" Mode.
    *   Option to turn off normal transfer.
*   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    :
    *   Renamed "Projection Origin" to "Base Height".
    *   "Base Height" no longer shows when in "Blend to Surface" mode.
    *   Fixed Cases where cutters would be flipped when generated causing several bugs.
    *   Refactored height calculation — should be no change to behavior.
    *   Added Debug for projection direction.
    *   Cleaned up all attributes created by modifier.
*   New Modifier: [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
    :
    *   Mirror-like modifier for single axis symmetry.
    *   Works with custom normals.
    *   Local/Global/Object space mirroring.
*   [Repair Mirrored Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
    :
    *   Added Mirror Object option.
*   New Modifier: [Change Materials](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/change_materials/)
    :
    *   Assign materials or material indices to face selections.
*   New Modifier: [Lock Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/lock_normals/)
    :
    *   Creates custom normals if none exist.
    *   Allows converting normals to "Tangent Space" which allows for deformation after setting.
*   New Manual Tool: [Add Custom Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/)
    :
    *   Tool version of "Lock Normals" for Object and Edit Mesh modes.
*   [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
    :
    *   Improving sharp edges appearing when transferring normals on small meshes.
*   [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
    :
    *   Fixed broken normals on very small meshes.

### Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/changelog/#add-on_1 "Permanent link")

*   Moved "Link Split Mesh" next to the "Split" Button.
*   Moved "Refresh" button above "Add Asset Library" to encourage use.
*   "Add Asset Library" now runs the "Refresh" operator to avoid adding the same library twice.
*   Fixed error when cutter collection is hidden/disabled.
*   Added "No Change" option to cutter display options.
*   Fixed cutters moving when using the redo panel. This occurred when parenting was on.
*   Now allows example file to live in the same folder as the asset library.

Individual Licenses[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/licensing/#individual-licenses "Permanent link")

Team / Studio Licenses[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/licensing/#team-studio-licenses "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

For teams/studios the number of people that use normalMagic is limited by the version purchased. The following team sizes are available:

*   Small Studio: up to 9 users.
*   Large Studio: 10-20 users.

For teams that exceed 20 users, additional licenses should be purchased. This only applies to the asset library's [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
. The licensing is as follows:

*   Asset Library: [Standard Royalty Free License](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license)
    .
*   Add-on: [GNU General Public License](https://www.gnu.org/licenses/gpl-3.0.html)
    .

Early Access Customers

For Studios that bought early access copies: individual licenses can be transferred into a studio license per request. This will discount the studio license by the price paid for each individual license.

Standard Royalty Free License[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/licensing/#standard-royalty-free-license "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Standard Royalty Free license grants you, the purchaser, the ability to make use of the purchased product for personal, educational, or commercial purposes as long as those purposes do not violate the following:

# Mesh Modifiers - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/#mesh-modifiers)

Mesh Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/#mesh-modifiers "Permanent link")

==============================================================================================================

These modifiers affect normals, underlying geometry and other attributes such as UVs.

*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    : Replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier with extra options and accurate normals.
*   [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    : Projects and blends mesh edges onto other mesh surfaces.
*   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    : Projects, cuts, blends and welds other mesh objects into the surface of a mesh.
*   [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
    : Single axis mirror modifier with extra options and works with custom normals.

# View Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#view-normals)

View Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#view-normals "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================================

[![View Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/view_normals_eye_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/view_normals_eye_1.png)

View Normals creates geometry to visualize mesh normals on objects.

*   Normals will show up in object mode
*   Normal Colors are currently only available in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
     and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
     viewport shading.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Length.** The object-space length of displayed normals
*   **Normal Domain.** Display normals on:
    *   **Points.** One line will be generated per point.
    *   **Face Corners.** One line will be generated per face corner.
    *   **Faces.** One line will be generated per face.
*   **View.** Specify which normal to visualize:
    *   **Normal.** Display the normal stored on the geometry, custom normals will be included here.
    *   **True Normal.** Display the normal defined by topology, custom normals will be ignored.
    *   **Attribute.** Specify an arbitrary attribute to use.

### Style[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#style "Permanent link")

This section defines how the normal line mesh is generated:

Line [![line](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/normal_line.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/normal_line.png)
 Display normals as a single line. This is the most efficient but can't be colored.

Cylinder / Tapered Cylinder [![line](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/normal_cylinder.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/normal_cylinder.png)
 Display Normals as mesh cylinders with a width and color that will show up in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
 and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
 viewport shading

#### Colors[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/#colors "Permanent link")

(When in Cylinder / Tapered Cylinder mode)

*   **Color.** Color to display normals.
*   **Show Default.** Colors normals differently if they match default "Smooth Shading" normals:
    
    [![Show Default](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/normal_default.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/normal_default.png)

---

# View Sharp - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#view-sharp)

View Sharp[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#view-sharp "Permanent link")

======================================================================================================================

[![View Sharp Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/view_sharp.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/view_sharp.png)

View sharp generates geometry to visualize sharp edges on a mesh. Similar to [View Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_normals/)
 colors are currently only available in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
 and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
 viewport shading.

[![View Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/view_sharp.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/view/view_sharp.gif)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#options "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Detect Sharp.** How to detect sharp edges:
    
    *   **From Markup.** Use smooth shading attributes to define sharp edges. This might miss sharp edges defined by custom normals.
    *   **From Normals.** Detect sharp edges from normals. This will detect sharp edges created from custom normals but can have a small amount of error.
    *   **Both.** Combine both methods above to detect sharp edges.
*   **Tolerance (When "From Normals" is used).** Change the normal angle difference at which edges will be marked as sharp.
    
*   **Width.** Width of pipe geometry created in object-space.
*   **Color.** Color to apply to sharp edge visualization.

---

# Instance Object - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/instance_object/#instance-object)

Instance Object[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/instance_object/#instance-object "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================

Instances another object (after modifiers) to replace or append to the current objects geometry. This can be particularly useful for re-using details.

[![Instance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/other_tools/instance.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/other_tools/instance.gif)
 Create duplicates of an object without needing to sync all modifier settings to the duplicates.

[![Instance Append](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/other_tools/instance_append.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/other_tools/instance_append.gif)
 Append to and nest modular pieces. Such as these bolt inserts, these can be instanced themselves and used with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 on larger meshes.

Add-on

The normalMagic [Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/)
 has instance operators to make working with this modifier easier:

[![addonpanel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)

*   Create a linked duplicate of an object(s), replace all modifiers with **Instance Object**
*   Select original object being instanced

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/instance_object/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Object.** Object to instance
*   **As Instance.** When turned off, will copy object's geometry. Turned on will instance the object [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/instances.html)
    
*   **Replace / Append.** Replace will replace current objects geometry with that of the instance. Append will keep geometry and append the instanced object with additional transform options.

### Transform Instance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/instance_object/#transform-instance "Permanent link")

When appending the instance, these settings allow you to transform the instance relative to the objects origin.

Mesh Modifiers[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/#mesh-modifiers "Permanent link")

==========================================================================================================

These modifiers effect normals, underlying geometry and other attributes such as UVs.

*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    . Replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier with extra options and accurate normals.
*   [Surface Blend](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_blend/)
    . Projects and blends mesh edges onto another mesh.
*   [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
    . Projects, cuts and welds other meshes into surface of mesh.

# Boolean Pro 🏗 - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro)

Boolean Pro ![🏗](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/jdecked/twemoji@15.1.0/assets/svg/1f3d7.svg ":construction_site:")[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro "Permanent link")

=====================================================================================================================================================================================================================

[![Boolean Pro Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)

Boolean Pro is designed to act as a replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier with extra functionality.

The most powerful feature is transferring the normals automatically from boolean inputs for perfect boolean normals:

[![Perfect Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)

Add-on

The normalMagic add-on (Pro version) includes operators to quickly set up the Boolean Pro modifier, similar the the Bool Tool add-on.

[normalMagic Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/add-on/)

# Auto-Flip Faces - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces)

Auto-Flip Faces[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================================================

[![Auto-Flip Faces Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)

Flipped faces on meshes can cause multiple issues. Traditionally you would check for flipped faces by using the the 'Face Orientation' overlay and resolve areas of flipped faces one by one.

This modifier will detect flipped faces and fix them automatically.

*   [![Faces Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)
     Faces Flipped
*   [![Faces Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)
     Faces Un-flipped

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Invert.** Change direction of entire mesh from outside to inside
*   **Hit Rate.** Tune the detection of flipped faces. Higher values will detect more but increase the risk of false positives.

# Repair Mirrored Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals)

Repair Mirrored Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals "Permanent link")

======================================================================================================================================================

Mirroring a mesh with custom normals will not mirror the normals. This leads to incorrect normals on the mirrored geometry.

This modifier will detect mesh islands with flipped normals and what axis they need flipping on.

*   [![Mirrored Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)
     Unmirrored Custom Normals
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)
     Mirrored Custom Normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#options "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges)
*   **Method.** Choose which faces get mirrored normals:
    *   **Detect.** Faces and axes will be determined automatically, this should work in most cases.
    *   **Specify.** Specify which faces to flip normals and which axes to flip them on.
