# Repair Boolean Normals 🏗 - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals)

Repair Boolean Normals ![🏗](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/jdecked/twemoji@15.1.0/assets/svg/1f3d7.svg ":construction_site:")[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals "Permanent link")

========================================================================================================================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Boolean Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)

This tool attempts to fix bad normals caused by booleans. This is intended for destructively applied booleans, for boolean modifiers please consider using [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 as it provides much more accurate and consistent results.

*   [![Bool Normals Default](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)
     Default Boolean Normals
*   [![Bool Normals Repaired](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)
     Repaired Boolean Normals

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Mesh object with destructive booleans applied.
2.  Mesh is un-triangulated i.e. boolean operations have created ngons.
3.  Mesh has sharp edges on boolean seams.

Even if a mesh meets all these requirements it isn't 100% guaranteed to fix all it's normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Error Correct Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#error-correct-distance "Permanent link")

Welds vertices on the proxy mesh that normals are transferred from.

This can fix normal issues but will also introduce more issues as the value increases. There should be a sweet spot where there aren't any issues

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/common_settings/#selection)
 options

How it Works[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#how-it-works "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Splitting the mesh on sharp edges, producing a surface for each "normal island"
2.  Detecting points on the boundary that don't join to any edges inside the island
3.  Re-creating a version of each island without those points
4.  Transferring normals per newly created island back up to the unaltered geometry

---

# Surface Insert 🏗 - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert)

Surface Insert ![🏗](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/jdecked/twemoji@15.1.0/assets/svg/1f3d7.svg ":construction_site:")[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert "Permanent link")

==============================================================================================================================================================================================================================

Seamlessly join **insert meshes** into a mesh's surface.  
An **insert mesh** can be thought of as a "decal", "patch" or "plug".

There are two primary ways of performing the insert operation:

*   [![Wrap to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)
     **Wrap to Surface**  
    Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)
     **Blend to Surface**  
    Keep world position of geometry and blend to surface via masking. This allows you to keep flat surfaces inside the insert mesh.

The **Surface Insert** Modifier performs the following steps:

1.  Insert meshes are projected to surface geometry using one of the above methods.
2.  Normals, materials and UVs are transferred from the original surface to the insert meshes.
3.  The surface is cut and removed where insert mesh open edges are.
4.  Vertices are matched on both sides of the seam and welded.

Mesh Islands

Insert meshes are performed per mesh island. Only mesh islands with open edges will be cut and welded into the original surface.

*   Manifold (solid) mesh islands can be projected but won't affect the underlying surface geometry.
*   Mesh islands that miss a raycast will be skipped entirely and a warning will show on the modifier.

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#requirements "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  A receiving mesh object. This will have the modifier on it.
2.  An insert mesh with open edges. This will be projected, cut and welded into the surface of the receiving mesh.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Insert Meshes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-meshes "Permanent link")

This section specifies which meshes to insert

*   **Objects/Collection.** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects
*   **Object Count.** How many mesh objects to insert (when in **_Objects_** mode)

### Insert Mesh Projection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-mesh-projection "Permanent link")

*   **Method.** How to project the insert meshes to the underlying surface
    
    *   **Wrap to Surface.** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
    *   **Blend with Surface.** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   **Projection Direction.** Which direction to project insert mesh(es)
    
    *   **Mesh Island Normal.** Use average mesh island normal
    *   **Insert Direction.** Specify a direction in the space of each insert object
    *   **Object Direction.** Specify a direction in the space of receiving object
*   **Projection Origin.** Where to use as the origin of the insert object. This will affect how the objects height is calculated for re-projection.
    
*   **Height Offset.** Offsets insert mesh away from surface by a specified height.
    
*   **Ray Length.** Maximum distance an insert mesh can have an effect.
    

#### Projection Masking[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#projection-masking "Permanent link")

This section controls masking the projection of insert meshes to the surface. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**. [![falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)
 Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

#### Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#normals "Permanent link")

This section controls how much of the surface normals are transferred to the insert mesh. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**.

*   **Normal Masking**
    
    *   **Projection Mask**. Use the same mask falloff as the projection
    *   **New Mask**. Create a new mask for normals
*   **Recalculate Blend Normals.** Only available when using **Blend with Surface**. This will recalculate the normals on the blended portion of the insert mesh before transferring any from the surface.
    
*   **Match Boundary Normals.** When choosing **_New Mask_** this will make sure the boundary edge of the insert mesh matches the normals on the underlying surface. Turn off to create a hard edge.

If **_New Mask_** is chosen, the settings for the falloff will appear below. Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

### Surface Boolean[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-boolean "Permanent link")

This section controls how the surface is cut for the insert mesh.

*   **Weld Surface.** Welds vertices between the surface and insert mesh. Turning this off will leave them as separate mesh islands.
*   **Weld Distance.** Distance at which to weld vertices, this takes effect at several points during the process, not just the final weld between surface and insert mesh.
*   **Boolean Extrusion.** How far to extrude the cutter shape from the surface. You might need to increase this value if the insert mesh covers a large area of curvature. You can preview cutters in the [Debug](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
     panel. [![Boolean Extrusion](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)
    
*   **Non-Manifold Solver.** Which boolean solver to use when surface mesh is not manifold. Keep this as **_Float_** for most situations.
    *   **Float.** Faster and in a lot of cases more accurate when matching surface points.
    *   **Exact.** Much slower and rarely better. Included "just in case" it helps in rare cases.

### Materials[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#materials "Permanent link")

This section controls how materials and UVs are transferred from the surface to the insert meshes

*   **Transfer Surface Material.** How to transfer materials from the surface to the insert mesh.
    
    *   **None.** Don't transfer any materials.
    *   **Boundary Material.** Transfer the material island(s) that touch open edges.
    *   **All.** Overwrite all insert mesh materials with those on the surface.
*   **Transfer Material UVs.** Transfer UV Maps for the transferred materials.
    
*   **UV Map.** Which UV map to transfer.

### Debug[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug "Permanent link")

This section lets you inspect certain points along the process if the modifier isn't behaving as expected.

*   **View.** Outputs geometry from different points in the node graph.
    
    Warning
    
    This changes the output of the graph, make sure to turn off when done inspecting.
    
    *   **None.** Output standard result, no debugging.
    *   **Boolean Cutters.** Output the shapes used to cut the surface.
    *   **Skipped Insert Islands.** Output any mesh islands that did not project due to missed ray casts.

*   [![Boolean Cutters](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)
     Debug Cutters
*   [![Skipped Islands](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)
     Debug Skipped Insert Islands

---

# Mark Sharp - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp)

Mark Sharp[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp "Permanent link")

==============================================================================================================

[![Mark Sharp Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)

Mark Sharp will mark edges as sharp based on edge angle. This is similar to Blender's [Smooth By Angle](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/normals/smooth_by_angle.html)
 modifier but with extra options. There is also the option to remove custom normals.

*   [![Smooth By Angle](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)
     **Smooth By Angle**  
    One angle for all edges
*   [![Mark Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)
     **Mark Sharp**  
    Separate angles for convex/concave edges

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/other_tools/mark_sharp/#options "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Keep Existing.** Keep edges marked as sharp.
*   **Angle Type.**
    *   **Absolute Angle.** Use a single angle regardless off convexity. This behaves like "Smooth By Angle".
    *   **Convex / Concave.** Specify different angles depending whether edges are convex or concave.
*   **Remove Custom Normals.** Will remove any custom normals from geometry and use default normal calculation from sharp edges.

---

# Repair Bevel Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals)

Repair Bevel Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Bevel Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)

Beveling a mesh with custom normals can lead to unexpected behavior. This tool identifies bevels and replicates Blender's "Harden Normals" functionality found on the bevel modifier.

*   [![default_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)
      
    Bevel with custom normals
*   [![repaired_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)
      
    Repaired Normals

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Mesh object with beveled faces you wish to fix
*   (Recommended) Set **Face Strength** to "Affected" or "All" on the bevel modifier
    
    [![Face Strength](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Bevel Detection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection "Permanent link")

How to detect bevelled faces.

*   **Face Strength.** Recommended option. Requires setting face strength on bevel modifier to "Affected" or "All"
    
    *   **Sharp Bevel Edge.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's outer edges.
    *   **Sharp Bevel Corners.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's corners.
*   **Face Area.** Select bevels by their face area.
    
    *   **Max Bevel Area.** Faces with area below this value will be treated as bevels
*   **Attribute.** Use an attribute or vertex group to select beveled faces.
    

### Area Weighting[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#area-weighting "Permanent link")

For weighting normals **inside** the bevel. Available when [Bevel Detection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection)
 is set to **Face Area** or **Attribute**

*   **Area Bias.** Bend normals towards faces with larger areas. 1 = Direct weight based on area. Higher values will weight more aggressively to larger areas.
*   **Area Cutoff.** Ignores normals from smaller faces. Cutoff is a percentage of face area compared to the largest connected face

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/common_settings/#selection)
 options

---

# Mark Sharp - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp)

Mark Sharp[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================

[![Mark Sharp Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)

Mark Sharp will mark edges as sharp based on edge angle. This is similar to Blender's [Smooth By Angle](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/normals/smooth_by_angle.html)
 modifier but with extra options. There is also the option to remove custom normals.

*   [![Smooth By Angle](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)
     **Smooth By Angle**  
    One angle for all edges
*   [![Mark Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)
     **Mark Sharp**  
    Separate angles for convex/concave edges

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/mark_sharp/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Keep Existing.** Keep edges marked as sharp.
*   **Angle Type.**
    *   **Absolute Angle.** Use a single angle regardless off convexity. This behaves like "Smooth By Angle".
    *   **Convex / Concave.** Specify different angles depending whether edges are convex or concave.
*   **Remove Custom Normals.** Will remove any custom normals from geometry and use default normal calculation from sharp edges.

---

# Surface Project - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#surface-project)

Surface Project[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#surface-project "Permanent link")

================================================================================================================================

[![Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/surface_blend_9.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/surface_blend_9.png)

Project and blend geometry onto other mesh surfaces. Transfer normals and UVs for a seamless blend.

*   [![Surface Project 1](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_1.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_1.gif)
      
    Blend a mesh object to other surfaces. Multiple other mesh surfaces can be specified.
    
*   [![Surface Project 2](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_2.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/surface_blend_2.gif)
      
    Project mesh as a "Decal" onto other objects. This flattens the geometry but keeps the original normals.
    

Open Edges

This modifier works best on meshes with "open edges", i.e. non-solid objects. These edges are used as the base for most [masking methods](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
.

[![open_edges](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/open_edges.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/open_edges.png)

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#examples "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Examples can be found in the **Surface Project/Insert** scene in the [example file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/examples/)
.

[![examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/examples/surface_project_insert.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#options "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Projection Targets[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#projection-targets "Permanent link")

Choose which meshes to project to.

*   **Target Objects:** How many mesh objects to project to.
*   **Objects:** Choose individual mesh objects to project to.
*   **Collection:** Choose a collection containing mesh objects to project to.

### Projection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#projection "Permanent link")

Define how mesh is projected to surfaces.

*   **Method:** Method for vertex projection: [![Method GIF](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/projection_methods.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_project/projection_methods.gif)
    *   **Wrap to Surface:** Wrap mesh to target surface. Points will be projected to target surface and re-displaced based on their calculated height.
    *   **Blend with Surface:** Project points to target surface then blend between original and projected positions.
    *   **Project Decal:** Flatten geometry to target surface while keeping original normals. Ideal for "floaters" intended for baking.
*   **Surface Offset:** Move projected vertices a fixed amount towards or away from the target surface.

#### Projection Direction[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#projection-direction "Permanent link")

Control the direction and properties of surface projection.

*   **Project Direction:** Define direction of projection rays:
    *   **Object Direction:** Direction in object space (default -Z).
    *   **World Direction:** Direction in world space (default -Z).
    *   **Mesh Normal:** Use individual point normals.
    *   **Mesh Island Normal:** Use the average normal of each mesh island.
    *   **Mesh Tangent:** Use individual point tangents.
    *   **Surface Normal:** Use the normal of the closest part of target surface.
*   **Base Height:** How height is calculated for **_Wrap to Surface_** and **_Project Decal_** projection methods:
    *   **Boundary Center:** Calculate height from a plane defined by the center of the boundary and the projection direction.
    *   **Object Origin:** Calculate height from a plane defined by the objects origin and the projection direction.
*   **Re-Displace:** When using **_Wrap to Surface_**. Direction to apply calculated height:
    *   **Projection:** Apply height displacement using the projection direction.
    *   **Surface Normal:** Apply height displacement using the normal of the target surface.
*   **Ray Length:** Maximum distance rays will collide with target surface. Best to keep at a large enough value that all points collide.
*   **No Hit:** Change behavior of vertices when projection rays miss:
    *   **Unchanged:** Do not move vertex.
    *   **Closest Surface to Max Dist:** Move vertex to the maximum distance in ray direction then snap to closest point on target surface.

#### Projection Masking[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#projection-masking "Permanent link")

Control how projection is masked. See [Mask Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
.

[![falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)

*   **Vertex Group:** Choose a vertex group to project.

### Transfer Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#transfer-normals "Permanent link")

Transfer normals from target surfaces. Internally this uses [Normal Transfer](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/)
. Normals are transferred after vertex projection, using the closest target surface.

*   **Normal Domain:** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Normal Masking:** How to mask the normal transfer:
    *   **Projection Mask:** Use the same mask used for vertex [projection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#projection-masking)
        .
    *   **New Mask:** Create a new mask using [mask falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
        .
*   **Vertex Group:** Limit all normal transfer to a vertex group.

### Transfer UVs[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/#transfer-uvs "Permanent link")

Transfer UVs from target surface.

*   **UV Map From:** Which UV Map to transfer from the **surface**.
*   **UV Map To:** Which UV Map to write to on this object.

---

# Instance Objects - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/instance_objects/#instance-objects)

Instance Objects[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/instance_objects/#instance-objects "Permanent link")

===============================================================================================================================

[![instance panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)

These tools make it easier to use the [Instance Object](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/instance_object/)
 modifier.

Duplicate Instanced[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/instance_objects/#duplicate-instanced "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This operator that acts like Blender's built-in [Duplicate Linked](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/scene_layout/object/editing/duplicate_linked.html)
 but also copies the results of all modifiers:

[![duplicate instanced](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/duplicate_instanced.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/duplicate_instanced.gif)

The **instanced** object can be duplicated with any existing methods e.g. Shift+D and will keep the same behavior.

Using this operator on an object that already is using a "Instance Object" as it's last modifier will instance the original object to keep things simple.

### What it does[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/instance_objects/#what-it-does "Permanent link")

For each selected object:

1.  Create a new object with the same mesh data as selected object. This is so you can still enter "Edit Mode" on the instance.
2.  Add [Instance Object](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/instance_object/)
     modifier and specify selected object.
3.  Select instance object and deselect original.

Select Original[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/instance_objects/#select-original "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Selects the original object(s) being instanced by selected object(s). This is useful when you wish to change modifier behavior on the original.

---

# Sharp Edges to Attribute - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edges-to-attribute)

Sharp Edges to Attribute[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edges-to-attribute "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================================================================

[![Sharp Edges to Attribute Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/sharp_to_attribute.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/sharp_to_attribute.png)

Write edge and point attributes based on sharp edges or other arbitrary edge attributes.

*   Edge attributes can be used for:
    
    *   Mark sharp edges as UV seams.
    *   Add a bevel weight to sharp edges.
    *   Detect sharp edges from custom normals and mark them as sharp.
*   Point attributes can be used for:
    
    *   Creating a vertex group for selection in another modifier from sharp edges.
    *   Expanding selection with or without a falloff.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#options "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Sharp Edge Detection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edge-detection "Permanent link")

How to specify edges to write to attribute(s)

*   **Detect From Markup.** Use smooth shading attributes to define sharp edges. This might miss sharp edges defined by custom normals.
*   **Detect Custom.** Detect sharp edges from normals. This will detect sharp edges created from custom normals but can have a small amount of error.
*   **Tolerance (When "Detect Custom" is on).** Change the normal angle difference at which edges will be marked as sharp.

### Expand Points[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#expand-points "Permanent link")

Expand point selection. Can be useful in [Selection Panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#selection)
 of other modifiers.

*   **Point Values.** How selection is expanded:
    *   **Float.** Each expansion gets a lower value creating a falloff.
    *   **Bool.** Each expansion is set to 1.
*   **Expand Iterations.** How many edges to expand point selection.
*   **Falloff (When Point Values are "Float").** Falloff of point selection. similar to [mask falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/common_settings/#base-falloff)
    

### Output Attributes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#output-attributes "Permanent link")

What to name Edge / Point attributes. **Leaving blank will not write to any attributes.**

---

# Boolean Pro - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro)

Boolean Pro[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro "Permanent link")

================================================================================================================

[![bpro panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)

These tools make it easier to use the [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 modifier.

Top Panel Operators[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#top-panel-operators "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The top of the panel contains operators for all Boolean Pro modes.

*   ### How to use[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#how-to-use "Permanent link")
    
    [![bool diff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/boolean_difference.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/boolean_difference.gif)
    
    **1.** Select cutter object(s).  
    **2.** Select the object you wish to cut.  
    **3.** Perform Boolean operation using the buttons.
    

### Operations[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#operations "Permanent link")

| Intersect | Union | Difference | Slice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [![Intersect](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png) | [![Union](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_union.png) | [![Difference](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png) | [![Slice](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif) |

### Split[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#split "Permanent link")

The "Split" Operator will duplicate the object:

*   The original will get a **Difference** Boolean
*   The duplicate will get a **Intersect** Boolean. There is an option under [Cutter Behavior](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#cutter-behavior)
     to make the intersection object linked.

Modifier Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#modifier-settings "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section lets you change what settings the Boolean Pro modifiers get **before being added** (Settings for existing modifiers won't be updated).

All options in this section are covered on the [Boolean Pro modifier](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 page.

Cutter Behavior[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#cutter-behavior "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section lets you choose what happens to objects when you add a Boolean.

*   **Display As.** Change the display mode of the cutter object:
    
    *   **Wireframe.** Display cutter as wireframe.
    *   **Bounds.** Display cutter as bounding box.
*   **Collection.** When enabled, specify the name of a collection to add cutter objects to. If the collection doesn't exist one will be created.
    
*   **Deselect.** Which objects get deselected after the boolean operation:
    *   **None.** Keep all objects selected.
    *   **Main Object.** Deselect the object that receives the modifier.
    *   **Cutters.** Deselect cutter object(s)
*   **Parent to Object.** Parent the cutter object(s) to the main object. This will transform them together when you move the main object.
*   **Link Split Mesh.** When performing the [Split](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#split)
     operation, link the intersected mesh to the original so editing one mesh edits the other too.
*   **Auto-Smooth.** Apply Blender's "Smooth By Angle" modifier to objects in Boolean operation if they don't have one already:
    *   **Cutters.** Apply smoothing to cutter object(s).
    *   **All.** Apply smoothing to all objects involved in the Boolean.
    *   **None.** Don't apply smoothing to any objects.
*   **Angle.** Angle to set auto-smoothing at when "Auto-Smooth" is turned on.

Convert Existing Booleans[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/#convert-existing-booleans "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Will convert all 'Boolean' modifiers on selected objects to 'Boolean Pro'. All relevant settings will be transferred.

---

# Auto-Flip Faces - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces)

Auto-Flip Faces[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================

[![Auto-Flip Faces Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)

Flipped faces on meshes can cause multiple issues. Traditionally you would check for flipped faces by using the the 'Face Orientation' overlay and resolve areas of flipped faces one by one.

This modifier will detect flipped faces and fix them automatically.

*   [![Faces Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)
     Faces Flipped
*   [![Faces Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)
     Faces Un-flipped

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Invert.** Change direction of entire mesh from outside to inside
*   **Hit Rate.** Tune the detection of flipped faces. Higher values will detect more but increase the risk of false positives.

---

# normalMagic Add-on - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/#normalmagic-add-on)

normalMagic Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/#normalmagic-add-on "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================

This add-on is included with the **Pro** version of normalMagic and makes working with certain modifiers easier.

*   [Install Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/install/#add-on)
    
*   [Preferences](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/preferences/)
    
*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/boolean_pro/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![bpro panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)
    
    Set up booleans using the Boolean Pro [modifier](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
     with one click and convert existing booleans.
    
*   [Instance Objects](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/add-on/instance_objects/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![instance panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)
    
    Duplicate objects as instances using the Instance Object [modifier](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.0/other_tools/instance_object/)
     with one click.

# Surface Insert - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert)

Surface Insert[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-insert "Permanent link")

=============================================================================================================================

[![Surface Insert Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/surface_insert6.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/surface_insert6.png)

Seamlessly join **insert meshes** into the surface of a mesh.

An **insert mesh** can be thought of as a "decal", "patch" or "plug".

There are two primary ways of performing the insert operation:

*   [![Wrap to Surface](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/wrap_to_surface.gif)
     **Wrap to Surface**  
    Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/blend_to_surface.gif)
     **Blend to Surface**  
    Keep world position of geometry and blend to surface via masking. This allows you to keep flat surfaces inside the insert mesh.

The **Surface Insert** Modifier performs the following steps:

1.  Insert meshes are projected to surface geometry using one of the above methods.
2.  Normals, materials and UVs are transferred from the original surface to the insert meshes.
3.  The surface is cut and removed where insert mesh open edges are.
4.  Vertices are matched on both sides of the seam and welded.

Mesh Islands

Insert meshes are performed per mesh island. Only mesh islands with **open edges** will be cut and welded into the original surface.

*   Manifold (solid) mesh islands can be projected but won't affect the underlying surface geometry.
*   Mesh islands that miss a raycast will be skipped entirely and a warning will show on the modifier.

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#requirements "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  A receiving mesh object. This will have the modifier on it.
2.  An insert mesh with open edges. This will be projected, cut and welded into the surface of the receiving mesh.
3.  Insert mesh cannot partially cut into surface, it has to be fully surrounded.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Insert Meshes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-meshes "Permanent link")

This section specifies which meshes to insert.

*   **Objects/Collection:** Choose individual mesh objects or a collection containing mesh objects.
*   **Object Count:** How many mesh objects to insert (when in **_Objects_** mode).

### Insert Mesh Projection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#insert-mesh-projection "Permanent link")

*   **Method:** How to project the insert meshes to the underlying surface:
    
    *   **Wrap to Surface:** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
    *   **Blend with Surface:** Warp geometry to match the underlying surface.
*   **Projection Direction:** Which direction to project insert mesh(es).
    
    *   **Mesh Island Normal:** Use average mesh island normal
    *   **Insert Direction:** Specify a direction in the space of each insert object.
    *   **Object Direction:** Specify a direction in the space of receiving object.
*   **Projection Origin.** Where to use as the origin of the insert object. This will affect how the objects height is calculated for re-projection.
    
*   **Height Offset.** Offsets insert mesh away from surface by a specified height.
    
*   **Ray Length.** Maximum distance an insert mesh can have an effect.
    

#### Projection Masking[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#projection-masking "Permanent link")

This section controls masking the projection of insert meshes to the surface. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**.

[![falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/falloff.gif)

Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

#### Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#normals "Permanent link")

This section controls how much of the surface normals are transferred to the insert mesh. This is most useful when using **_Blend with Surface_**.

*   **Normal Masking**
    
    *   **Projection Mask**. Use the same mask falloff as the projection
    *   **New Mask**. Create a new mask for normals
*   **Recalculate Blend Normals.** Only available when using **Blend with Surface**. This will recalculate the normals on the blended portion of the insert mesh before transferring any from the surface.
    
*   **Match Boundary Normals.** When choosing **_New Mask_** this will make sure the boundary edge of the insert mesh matches the normals on the underlying surface. Turn off to create a hard edge.

If **_New Mask_** is chosen, the settings for the falloff will appear below. Controlling falloff is covered [here](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#mask-falloff)

### Surface Boolean[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#surface-boolean "Permanent link")

This section controls how the surface is cut for the insert mesh.

*   **Weld Surface.** Welds vertices between the surface and insert mesh. Turning this off will leave them as separate mesh islands.
*   **Weld Distance.** Distance at which to weld vertices, this takes effect at several points during the process, not just the final weld between surface and insert mesh.
*   **Boolean Extrusion.** How far to extrude the cutter shape from the surface. You might need to increase this value if the insert mesh covers a large area of curvature. You can preview cutters in the [Debug](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
     panel. [![Boolean Extrusion](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/boolean_extrusion.gif)
    
*   **Boolean Offset.** Offset the Boolean cutter outside the surface. This only effects the "top" of the cutter and will also help when an insert mesh covers a large area of curvature. You can preview cutters in the [Debug](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug)
     panel.
*   **Non-Manifold Solver.** Which boolean solver to use when surface mesh is not manifold. Keep this as **_Float_** for most situations.
    *   **Float.** Faster and in a lot of cases more accurate when matching surface points.
    *   **Exact.** Much slower and rarely better. Included "just in case" it helps in rare cases.

### Materials[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#materials "Permanent link")

This section controls how materials and UVs are transferred from the surface to the insert meshes

*   **Transfer Surface Material.** How to transfer materials from the surface to the insert mesh.
    
    *   **None.** Don't transfer any materials.
    *   **Boundary Material.** Transfer the material island(s) that touch open edges.
    *   **All.** Overwrite all insert mesh materials with those on the surface.
*   **Transfer Material UVs.** Transfer UV Maps for the transferred materials.
    
*   **UV Map.** Which UV map to transfer.

### Debug[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/#debug "Permanent link")

This section lets you inspect certain points along the process if the modifier isn't behaving as expected.

*   **View.** Outputs geometry from different points in the node graph.
    
    Warning
    
    This changes the output of the graph, make sure to turn off when done inspecting.
    
    *   **None.** Output standard result, no debugging.
    *   **Boolean Cutters.** Output the shapes used to cut the surface.
    *   **Skipped Insert Islands.** Output any mesh islands that did not project due to missed ray casts.

*   [![Boolean Cutters](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/debug_cutters.png)
     Debug Cutters
*   [![Skipped Islands](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/surface_insert/debug_skipped_islands.png)
     Debug Skipped Insert Islands

---

# Boolean Pro - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro)

Boolean Pro[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================

[![Boolean Pro Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/boolean_cut_7_sparkles.png)

Boolean Pro is designed to act as a replacement for Blender's built-in boolean modifier, and provides extra functionality. For reference, documentation for Blender's built-in modifier can be found [here](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/booleans.html)
.

Add-on

The [normalMagic Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/)
 includes operators to quickly set up the Boolean Pro modifier, similar to the Bool Tool add-on.

The most powerful feature is transferring the normals automatically from boolean inputs for perfect boolean normals:

[![Perfect Boolean Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bool_pro_1.gif)

The cutter object can be given an outset (expanded) with even thickness before cutting:

[![Expand Boolean](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_expand.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_expand.gif)

There is also a **_Slice_** operation with optional inset:

| Intersect | Union | Difference | Slice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [![Intersect](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png) | [![Union](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_union.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_union.png) | [![Difference](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png) | [![Slice](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif) |

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Main[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#main "Permanent link")

*   **Operation:** Which boolean operation to perform.
    *   **Intersect:** Keep the part of the mesh that is shared between inputs.
    *   **Union:** Combine input meshes into a single mesh.
    *   **Difference:** (Default) Subtract input meshes from source mesh.
    *   **Slice:** Slice source mesh with input meshes. This combines "Intersect" and "Difference" into the same output.
*   **Object:** Mesh object to use for boolean operation.
*   **Collection:** Collection containing mesh objects to use for boolean operation.
*   **Solver:**
    *   **Float:** Simple solver with good performance.
    *   **Exact:** Slower solver with best results on coplanar faces.
    *   **Manifold:** (Default) Fastest solver that only works on manifold meshes, but gives better results.
*   **Outset:** Expands the cutter mesh with even thickness.
*   **Inset (Slice Mode):** Offsets the cutter inwards for the intersect boolean to give a gap between meshes.

### Weld[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#weld "Permanent link")

*   **Merge by Distance:** Merge vertices based on distance.
*   **Distance:** Distance at which to merge vertices.

### Shading[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#shading "Permanent link")

*   **Materials:** How to assign materials to new faces.
    *   **Index Based:** Match material index from boolean meshes.
    *   **Specify:** Specify a material to apply to all new faces.
    *   **Transfer:** Transfer materials from boolean meshes. This might not be accurate when using collections.
*   **Transfer Normals:** Use normals from input meshes. Produces the best normals.
*   **Mark Sharp:** Mark all intersection edges between meshes as sharp.

### Solver Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/#solver-options "Permanent link")

*   **Result:** Whether to output a solid object or a cut surface.
    *   **Solid:** Output full Boolean result.
    *   **Surface:** Output a cut surface with open edges where new faces would appear.
*   **Sequential (Float solver only):** Perform Booleans one mesh island at a time. This often gives better results but can be slower.
*   **Self Intersection (Exact solver only):** Correctly calculates cases when one or both operands have self-intersections. This involves more calculations making the node slower.
*   **Hole Tolerant:** Optimizes the Boolean output for non-manifold geometry at the cost of increased computational time. Because of the performance impact, this option should only be enabled when the solver demonstrates errors with non-manifold geometry.

---

# Tools Overview 🏗 - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/tools_overview/#tools-overview)

Tools Overview ![🏗](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/jdecked/twemoji@15.1.0/assets/svg/1f3d7.svg ":construction_site:")[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/tools_overview/#tools-overview "Permanent link")

===================================================================================================================================================================================================================

normalMagic is comprised of several tools. Some modify normals, some modify geometry and normals, and some visualize normals and related attributes.

Custom Normals

Most of these tools create or edit **_custom normals_**. These can be unintuitive to work with if you're unfamiliar Once custom normals exist on a mesh, some default Blender tools/modifiers might not behave as expected. Tools are included to fix situations such as [mirroring](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/)
 or [beveling](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/)
 but it's something to be aware of.

---

# normalMagic Add-on - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/add-on/#normalmagic-add-on)

normalMagic Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/add-on/#normalmagic-add-on "Permanent link")

==============================================================================================================

---

# Symmetrize - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#symmetrize)

Symmetrize[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#symmetrize "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/symmetrize.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/symmetrize.png)

Mirrors a mesh on a single axis. Similar to Blender's [Mirror Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/generate/mirror.html)
 but with the following improvements:

1.  Correctly mirrors custom normals.
2.  Optional gizmo for displaying the symmetry plane and direction.
3.  Output attribute for mirrored faces.
4.  Global space option without having to use another object.

[![Symmetrize GIF](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/symmetrize/symmetrize.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/symmetrize/symmetrize.gif)

The one disadvantage of Symmetrize is the limitation to a single axis. However, this can easily be overcome by using another symmetrize modifier.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Symmetry Space:** Coordinate space to use for symmetry.
    *   **Local:** Symmetrize in object's local space (default mirror modifier behavior).
    *   **Global:** Symmetrize in global space based on the world origin.
    *   **Object:** Symmetrize using another objects position and rotation.
*   **Axis:** Which axis to symmetrize on, relative to the **_Symmetry Space_**.
*   **Flip Axis:** Reverse direction of symmetry operation.
*   **Show Axis:** Display location and direction of symmetry operation.
*   **Clipping:** Clip geometry on the mirrored side of the symmetry plane.
    *   **None:** No Clipping. All geometry will be mirrored across the symmetry plane.
    *   **Snap Points:** Points on the mirrored side will be snapped to the symmetry plane.
    *   **Cut Geometry:** Geometry will be sliced on the symmetry plane.

### Merge Points[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#merge-points "Permanent link")

Merge points by distance after symmetry operation.

### UVs[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#uvs "Permanent link")

Options to flip and offset UVs.

*   **UV Map:** Name of UV Map to edit.
*   **Flip U:** Flip mirrored UVs horizontally.
*   **Flip V:** Flip mirrored UVs vertically.
*   **Offset:** Offset mirrored UVs.

### Center Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/#center-normals "Permanent link")

*   **Mark Sharp:** Mark center edges as sharp based on angle.
*   **Angle:** Minimum angle for a sharp edge.
*   **Unify Custom Normals:** Face corner normals on center points will average. Sharp edges will be preserved. Only applies if custom normals exist on mesh.
