# View Sharp - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#view-sharp)

View Sharp[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#view-sharp "Permanent link")

======================================================================================================================

[![View Sharp Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/view_sharp.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/view_sharp.png)

View sharp generates geometry to visualize sharp edges on a mesh. Similar to [View Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_normals/)
 colors are currently only available in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
 and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
 viewport shading.

[![View Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/view_sharp.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/view_sharp.gif)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_sharp/#options "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Detect Sharp.** How to detect sharp edges:
    
    *   **From Markup.** Use smooth shading attributes to define sharp edges. This might miss sharp edges defined by custom normals.
    *   **From Normals.** Detect sharp edges from normals. This will detect sharp edges created from custom normals but can have a small amount of error.
    *   **Both.** Combine both methods above to detect sharp edges.
*   **Tolerance (When "From Normals" is used).** Change the normal angle difference at which edges will be marked as sharp.
    
*   **Width.** Width of pipe geometry created in object-space.
*   **Color.** Color to apply to sharp edge visualization.

---

# Lock Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/lock_normals/#lock-normals)

Lock Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/lock_normals/#lock-normals "Permanent link")

=========================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/lock_normals.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/lock_normals.png)

Creates custom normals for a mesh if none exist. Can also be used to convert between "Free" and "Tangent Space" normals, see the [Blender Docs](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/mesh/write/set_mesh_normal.html#properties)
:

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/lock_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Custom Normal Type:** How to store custom normals:
    
    *   **Free:** Store custom normals as simple vectors in the local space of the mesh. This is efficient and fast to evaluate but does not support deformation.
    *   **Tangent Space:** Store normals in a deformation dependent custom transformation space. This method is slower, but can be better when subsequent operations change the mesh without handling normals specifically.
*   **Normal Domain:** When **_Custom Normal Type_** is set to **_Free_**:
    
    *   **Face Corner.** Store normals on face corners, allows for sharp edges.
    *   **Point.** Store normals on points, cannot have sharp edges.

---

# Mark Sharp - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp)

Mark Sharp[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/mark_sharp/#mark-sharp "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================

[![Mark Sharp Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/mark_sharp.png)

Mark Sharp will mark edges as sharp based on edge angle. This is similar to Blender's [Smooth By Angle](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/normals/smooth_by_angle.html)
 modifier but with extra options. There is also the option to remove custom normals.

*   [![Smooth By Angle](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mark_sharp_off.png)
     **Smooth By Angle**  
    One angle for all edges
*   [![Mark Sharp](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mark_sharp_on.png)
     **Mark Sharp**  
    Separate angles for convex/concave edges

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/mark_sharp/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Keep Existing.** Keep edges marked as sharp.
*   **Angle Type.**
    *   **Absolute Angle.** Use a single angle regardless off convexity. This behaves like "Smooth By Angle".
    *   **Convex / Concave.** Specify different angles depending whether edges are convex or concave.
*   **Remove Custom Normals.** Will remove any custom normals from geometry and use default normal calculation from sharp edges.

---

# Auto-Flip Faces - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces)

Auto-Flip Faces[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#auto-flip-faces "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================

[![Auto-Flip Faces Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/auto-flip.png)

Flipped faces on meshes can cause multiple issues. Traditionally you would check for flipped faces by using the the 'Face Orientation' overlay and resolve areas of flipped faces one by one.

This modifier will detect flipped faces and fix them automatically.

*   [![Faces Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/suzanne_flipped.png)
     Faces Flipped
*   [![Faces Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/suzanne_unflipped.png)
     Faces Un-flipped

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/auto-flip_faces/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Invert.** Change direction of entire mesh from outside to inside
*   **Hit Rate.** Tune the detection of flipped faces. Higher values will detect more but increase the risk of false positives.

---

# Recalculate Weighted - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/manual_tools/recalculate_weighted/#recalculate-weighted)

Recalculate Weighted[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/manual_tools/recalculate_weighted/#recalculate-weighted "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================================

[![recalculate_weighted](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/recalculate_normals.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/recalculate_normals.png)

Calculated weighted normals for selected geometry in "Edit Mode". This is a tool version of the [Face Weighted Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/)
 modifier.

This can be found in the **Normal Magic** Menu when editing meshes.

---

# Add Custom Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/#add-custom-normals)

Add Custom Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/#add-custom-normals "Permanent link")

===========================================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/add_custom_normals.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/add_custom_normals.png)

Adds custom normals to selected mesh object. Internally uses the [Lock Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/lock_normals/)
 tool.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/manual_tools/add_custom_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Type:** How to store custom normals:
    
    *   **Free:** Store custom normals as simple vectors in the local space of the mesh. This is efficient and fast to evaluate but does not support deformation.
    *   **Tangent Space:** Store normals in a deformation dependent custom transformation space. This method is slower, but can be better when subsequent operations change the mesh without handling normals specifically.
*   **Normal Domain:** When **_Custom Normal Type_** is set to **_Free_**:
    
    *   **Face Corner.** Store normals on face corners, allows for sharp edges.
    *   **Point.** Store normals on points, cannot have sharp edges.

---

# Face Weighted Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#face-weighted-normals)

Face Weighted Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#face-weighted-normals "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================================================

[![Face Weighted Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/weighted_normals2.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/weighted_normals2.png)

Calculate face weighted normals for a mesh. This is similar to Blender's built-in [Weighted Normals Modifier](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/modifiers/normals/weighted_normal.html)
 but with some differences:

*   Selection Panel lets you limit weighted normals to a specific part of your mesh.
*   Options are designed to be more intuitive.

"Corner Angle" weighting is not yet implemented

This can create some odd shading on certain topology and is planned for a future update.

*   [![weighted off](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/weighted/weighted_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/weighted/weighted_off.png)
      
    Un-weighted normals
    
*   [![weighted on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/weighted/weighted_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/weighted/weighted_on.png)
      
    Weighted normals
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Weight.** Derive normal weights from:
    *   **Face Area.** Faces with larger area have more influence over normals.
    *   **Face Strength.** Use the "Face Strength" attribute. [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/meshes/editing/mesh/normals.html#set-face-strength)
        
    *   **Attribute.** Define an attribute to use for normal weighting.
*   **Keep Sharp.** Keep sharp edges on face corners.

### Area Weights[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#area-weights "Permanent link")

Have more control over area-weighted normals.

*   **Area Bias.** Bend normals towards faces with larger areas. 1 = Direct weight based on area. Higher values will weight more aggressively to larger areas.
*   **Area Cutoff.** Ignores normals from smaller faces. Cutoff is a percentage of face area compared to the largest connected face.

Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/face_weighted_normals/#selection "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection)
 options.

---

# Normal Transfer - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#normal-transfer)

Normal Transfer[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#normal-transfer "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================================

[![Normal Transfer Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/normal_transfer_4.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/normal_transfer_4.png)

Transfer Normals from other mesh objects. This can be useful in a variety of situations:

*   [![blend](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/blend.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/blend.gif)
      
    Blend between meshes without affecting geometry.
    
*   [![fix](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/fix.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/fix.gif)
      
    Fix / smooth shading of bad topology. See also [Smooth Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/)
     and [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
    
*   [![foliage](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/foliage.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/foliage.gif)
      
    Create better shading for foliage cards.
    
*   [![floater](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/floater.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/normal_transfer/floater.gif)
     Match normals of "floater" decals. See also [Surface Project](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_project/)
    

Examples[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#examples "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Examples can be found in the **Normal Modifiers** scene in the [example file](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/examples/)

[![examples](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/examples/normal_modifiers.png)

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).

### Transfer Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#transfer-geometry "Permanent link")

Choose which meshes to transfer normals from.

*   **Object Count.** How many mesh objects to transfer normals from.
*   **Objects.** Choose individual mesh objects to transfer normals from.
*   **Collection.** Choose a collection containing mesh objects to transfer normals from.

### Transfer[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#transfer "Permanent link")

*   **Method.** How to sample the normal from target surface:
    
    *   **Closest Surface.** Sample normal at closest target surface.
    *   **Project Object Direction.** Raycast-sample target geometry in object space.
    *   **Project Island Normal.** Raycast-sample target geometry along the average mesh island normal.
    *   **Project Source Normals.** Raycast-sample target geometry along mesh normals of this object.
*   **Ray Length (Project Methods).** Maximum length of raycast to target surface.
    
*   **Direction (Project Object Direction Only).** Direction in object space to raycast (default is -Z).
    
*   **Automatic Masking.** Mask falloff for transferring normals, see [Mask Falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#mask-falloff)
    

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/normal_transfer/#selection "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection)
 options.

---

# Smooth Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals)

Smooth Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals "Permanent link")

===============================================================================================================================

[![Smooth Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/smooth_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/smooth_normals_1.png)

Smooth Normals will smooth mesh normals without smoothing mesh topology. This can be useful in a variety of situations.

*   [![fix_bad_shading](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/fix%20bad_shading.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/fix%20bad_shading.gif)
     Achieve good shading on difficult topology. (Pinched, uneven or distorted normals).
    
*   [![cel_shading](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/cel_contour.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/cel_contour.gif)
     Simplify cel shading contours.
    
*   [![smooth_flatten](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/smooth_flatten.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/smooth_flatten.gif)
     Smooth and/or completely flatten normal islands.
    
*   [![mix_point_normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/mix_point_normals.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/mix_point_normals.gif)
     Mix smoothed point normals for a unique effect (cheap SSS shading).
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain.** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges).
*   **Keep Sharp.** Keep edges marked as sharp when normals are on face corners.

### Smoothed Proxy Mesh[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smoothed-proxy-mesh "Permanent link")

Generate a smoothed version of the mesh to transfer normals from. The mesh is split on sharp edges before smoothing, you can preview this mesh from the debug panel.

[![smoothed_proxy](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/smoothed_proxy.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/smooth/smoothed_proxy.gif)

*   **Transfer Method.** How to transfer the normals from the smoothed mesh.
    *   **Closest Surface.** Transfer normals from the closest point of the smoothed mesh.
    *   **Point Index.** Transfer normals by their original point index.
*   **Iterations.** How many iterations of smoothing to perform.

### Smooth Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#smooth-normals_1 "Permanent link")

Controls methods of directly smoothing mesh normals.

*   **Smooth Method.** How to smooth normals:
    
    *   **None.** Don't perform any direct normal smoothing.
    *   **Blur Normals.** Fastest Method of smoothing at the cost of being less accurate and not preserving shape as well.
    *   **Smooth Normals.** Iterative smooth function of normals. Slower than blur but can produce better results.
*   **Smooth/Blur Iterations.** How many iterations of smoothing to apply to normals.
    
*   **Face Weight Influence.** Cheap approximation of face weighted normals, lessens the contribution of smaller faces.

### Flatten Normal Islands[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#flatten-normal-islands "Permanent link")

Calculate the average normal direction for entire normal islands (defined by sharp edges).

*   **Amount.** How much to blend to the completely flat island normals.

### Mix Point Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#mix-point-normals "Permanent link")

Mix in smoothed point normals. This can achieve a unique effect, somewhat like sub-surface scattering.

*   **Amount.** How much to blend to the smoothed point normals.
*   **Blur Iterations.** Perform blur iterations on the point normals.

### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/smooth_normals/#selection "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection)
 options

---

# Bent Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/bent_normals/#bent-normals)

Bent Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/bent_normals/#bent-normals "Permanent link")

=========================================================================================================================

[![Bent Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/bent_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/bent_normals_1.png)

Bent normals can improve how surfaces react to ambient lighting, especially in non-path-traced renderers such as game engines.

Usually bent normals are baked to a texture but this modifier will calculate bent normals **directly on geometry**. A cone of rays is cast from each point and the normals are sampled and averaged to find the average direction of ambient light.

*   [![bent off](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bent/bent_off.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bent/bent_off.png)
      
    **Bent normals off**  
    Unnaturally dark shadows.
    
*   [![bent on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bent/bent_on.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bent/bent_on.png)
      
    **Bent normals on**  
    Fake bounce light added to shadow areas.
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/bent_normals/#options "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Factor.** How much bent normals are mixed in with existing normals.

### Collision Geometry[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/bent_normals/#collision-geometry "Permanent link")

Collide with other objects in the scene.

*   **Object.** Object to collide rays with.
*   **Collection.** Collection to collide rays with.

### RayCast[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/bent_normals/#raycast "Permanent link")

Control the distribution of rays used to calculate the bent normals.

*   **Ray Count.** How many rays are cast.
*   **Ray Length.** How long to cast each ray.
*   **Cone Angle.** The angle of the cone that rays are distributed in using a Fibonacci distribution.

---

# Repair Bevel Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals)

Repair Bevel Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#repair-bevel-normals "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Bevel Normals Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/repair_bevel.png)

Beveling a mesh with custom normals can lead to unexpected behavior. This tool identifies bevels and replicates Blender's "Harden Normals" functionality found on the bevel modifier.

*   [![default_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bevel_custom_default.png)
      
    Bevel with custom normals
*   [![repaired_bevel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bevel_custom_repaired.png)
      
    Repaired Normals

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Mesh object with beveled faces you wish to fix
*   (Recommended) Set **Face Strength** to "Affected" or "All" on the bevel modifier
    
    [![Face Strength](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/bevel_face_strength.png)
    

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Bevel Detection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection "Permanent link")

How to detect bevelled faces.

*   **Face Strength.** Recommended option. Requires setting face strength on bevel modifier to "Affected" or "All"
    
    *   **Sharp Bevel Edge.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's outer edges.
    *   **Sharp Bevel Corners.** Apply sharp edges to bevel's corners.
*   **Face Area.** Select bevels by their face area.
    
    *   **Max Bevel Area.** Faces with area below this value will be treated as bevels
*   **Attribute.** Use an attribute or vertex group to select beveled faces.
    

### Area Weighting[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#area-weighting "Permanent link")

For weighting normals **inside** the bevel. Available when [Bevel Detection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#bevel-detection)
 is set to **Face Area** or **Attribute**

*   **Area Bias.** Bend normals towards faces with larger areas. 1 = Direct weight based on area. Higher values will weight more aggressively to larger areas.
*   **Area Cutoff.** Ignores normals from smaller faces. Cutoff is a percentage of face area compared to the largest connected face

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_bevel_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection)
 options

---

# Repair Boolean Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals)

Repair Boolean Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#repair-boolean-normals "Permanent link")

=======================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Boolean Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/repair_boolean_normals_1.png)

This tool attempts to fix bad normals caused by booleans. This is intended for destructively applied booleans, for boolean modifiers please consider using [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 as it provides much more accurate and consistent results.

*   [![Bool Normals Default](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_unrepaired.png)
     Default Boolean Normals
*   [![Bool Normals Repaired](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_repaired.png)
     Repaired Boolean Normals

Requirements[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#requirements "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Mesh object with destructive booleans applied.
2.  Mesh is un-triangulated i.e. boolean operations have created ngons.
3.  Mesh has sharp edges on boolean seams.

Even if a mesh meets all these requirements it isn't 100% guaranteed to fix all it's normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#options "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Error Correct Distance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#error-correct-distance "Permanent link")

Welds vertices on the proxy mesh that normals are transferred from.

This can fix normal issues but will also introduce more issues as the value increases. There should be a sweet spot where there aren't any issues

### Selection Panel[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#selection-panel "Permanent link")

See [Selection](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection)
 options

How it Works[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_boolean_normals/#how-it-works "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Splitting the mesh on sharp edges, producing a surface for each "normal island"
2.  Detecting points on the boundary that don't join to any edges inside the island
3.  Re-creating a version of each island without those points
4.  Transferring normals per newly created island back up to the unaltered geometry

---

# Repair Mirrored Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals)

Repair Mirrored Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#repair-mirrored-normals "Permanent link")

==========================================================================================================================================================

[![Repair Mirrored Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/repair_mirrored.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/repair_mirrored.png)

Mirroring a mesh with custom normals will not mirror the normals. This leads to incorrect normals on the mirrored geometry.

This modifier can repair these normals by flipping them in the correct direction. Alternatively the [Symmetrize](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/symmetrize/)
 modifier is a better option when only mirroring on one axis.

*   [![Mirrored Flipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mirrored_flipped.png)
     Unmirrored Custom Normals
*   [![Mirrored Unflipped](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/mirrored_unflipped.png)
     Mirrored Custom Normals

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#options "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Domain:** Whether normals are stored on points (smooth) or face corners (allows sharp edges)
*   **Method:** Choose which faces get mirrored normals:
    *   **Detect:** Faces and axes will be determined automatically, this should work in most cases.
    *   **[Side of Origin:](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#side-of-origin)
        ** Select and mirror normals based on axis and object origin.
    *   **[Specify:](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#specify)
        ** Specify which faces to flip normals and which axes to flip them on.
*   **Mirror Object:** If using a mirror object, specify it here.

### Side of Origin[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#side-of-origin "Permanent link")

Flip normals for faces on the "mirrored" side. Use this method when using "Bisect" on a mirror modifier or when original geometry doesn't cross the mirror plane(s).

*   **Recalculate Center Normals:** Recalculate Normals for points at the center of specified axes.
*   **Mirror Axis:** Which axes to select and mirror normals on.
*   **Flip Axis:** Flip the direction of the selection of normals to mirror.

### Specify[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/normal_tools/repair_mirrored_normals/#specify "Permanent link")

Choose which faces have flipped normals and what axis to flip them on. Use when geometry is mirrored across both sides of the mirror plane(s) and "Bisect" is turned off.

*   **Mirror Axis:** Which axes to select and mirror normals on.
*   **Selection:** See [Selection Options](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection)

---

# View Normals - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_normals/#view-normals)

View Normals[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_normals/#view-normals "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================================

[![View Normals](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/view_normals_eye_1.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/view_normals_eye_1.png)

View Normals creates geometry to visualize mesh normals on objects.

*   Normals will show up in object mode
*   Normal Colors are currently only available in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
     and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
     viewport shading.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_normals/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Length.** The object-space length of displayed normals
*   **Normal Domain.** Display normals on:
    *   **Points.** One line will be generated per point.
    *   **Face Corners.** One line will be generated per face corner.
    *   **Faces.** One line will be generated per face.
*   **View.** Specify which normal to visualize:
    *   **Normal.** Display the normal stored on the geometry, custom normals will be included here.
    *   **True Normal.** Display the normal defined by topology, custom normals will be ignored.
    *   **Attribute.** Specify an arbitrary attribute to use.

### Style[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_normals/#style "Permanent link")

This section defines how the normal line mesh is generated:

Line [![line](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/normal_line.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/normal_line.png)
 Display normals as a single line. This is the most efficient but can't be colored.

Cylinder / Tapered Cylinder [![line](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/normal_cylinder.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/normal_cylinder.png)
 Display Normals as mesh cylinders with a width and color that will show up in [Material Preview](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#material-preview)
 and [Rendered](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/editors/3dview/display/shading.html#rendered)
 viewport shading

#### Colors[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/visualize_tools/view_normals/#colors "Permanent link")

(When in Cylinder / Tapered Cylinder mode)

*   **Color.** Color to display normals.
*   **Show Default.** Colors normals differently if they match default "Smooth Shading" normals:
    
    [![Show Default](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/normal_default.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/view/normal_default.png)

---

# Change Materials - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/change_materials/#change-materials)

Change Materials[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/change_materials/#change-materials "Permanent link")

====================================================================================================================================

[![Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/change_materials.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/change_materials.png)

Assign materials or material indices to face selections.

[![Demo](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/change_materials/change_materials_demo.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/change_materials/change_materials_demo.gif)

Up to 6 materials can be assigned per modifier. Selections can be driven by existing material/index or arbitrary vertex/face groups.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/change_materials/#options "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Materials to Apply:** The number of materials to apply. This will show a panel for each material with a checkbox to enable/disable.

### Material \*[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/change_materials/#material "Permanent link")

Panel for each material assignment, contains two panels: One for selecting faces and one for specifying material/index.

#### Selection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/change_materials/#selection "Permanent link")

Choose which faces to apply a material to.

*   **Face Group:** Specify a vertex/face group to apply materials to.
*   **Material:** Select faces by a specific material.
*   **Material Index:** Select faces by their material index.
*   **Invert:** Invert selection

#### Material[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/change_materials/#material_1 "Permanent link")

Which material to apply to selected faces.

*   **Set:** Whether to set a specific material or material index.
*   **Material:** Which specific material to apply.
*   **Material Index:** Which material index to apply.

---

# Sharp Edges to Attribute - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edges-to-attribute)

Sharp Edges to Attribute[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edges-to-attribute "Permanent link")

============================================================================================================================================================

[![Sharp Edges to Attribute Icon](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/sharp_to_attribute.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/icons/sharp_to_attribute.png)

Write edge and point attributes based on sharp edges or other arbitrary edge attributes.

*   Edge attributes can be used for:
    
    *   Mark sharp edges as UV seams.
    *   Add a bevel weight to sharp edges.
    *   Detect sharp edges from custom normals and mark them as sharp.
*   Point attributes can be used for:
    
    *   Creating a vertex group for selection in another modifier from sharp edges.
    *   Expanding selection with or without a falloff.

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#options "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Sharp Edge Detection[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#sharp-edge-detection "Permanent link")

How to specify edges to write to attribute(s)

*   **Detect From Markup.** Use smooth shading attributes to define sharp edges. This might miss sharp edges defined by custom normals.
*   **Detect Custom.** Detect sharp edges from normals. This will detect sharp edges created from custom normals but can have a small amount of error.
*   **Tolerance (When "Detect Custom" is on).** Change the normal angle difference at which edges will be marked as sharp.

### Expand Points[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#expand-points "Permanent link")

Expand point selection. Can be useful in [Selection Panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#selection)
 of other modifiers.

*   **Point Values.** How selection is expanded:
    *   **Float.** Each expansion gets a lower value creating a falloff.
    *   **Bool.** Each expansion is set to 1.
*   **Expand Iterations.** How many edges to expand point selection.
*   **Falloff (When Point Values are "Float").** Falloff of point selection. similar to [mask falloff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/common_settings/#base-falloff)
    

### Output Attributes[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/sharp_edges_to_attribute/#output-attributes "Permanent link")

What to name Edge / Point attributes. **Leaving blank will not write to any attributes.**

---

# Instance Objects - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/instance_objects/#instance-objects)

Instance Objects[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/instance_objects/#instance-objects "Permanent link")

===============================================================================================================================

[![instance panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)

These tools make it easier to use the [Instance Object](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/instance_object/)
 modifier.

Duplicate Instanced[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/instance_objects/#duplicate-instanced "Permanent link")

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This operator that acts like Blender's built-in [Duplicate Linked](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/scene_layout/object/editing/duplicate_linked.html)
 but also copies the results of all modifiers:

[![duplicate instanced](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/duplicate_instanced.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/duplicate_instanced.gif)

The **instanced** object can be duplicated with any existing methods e.g. Shift+D and will keep the same behavior.

Using this operator on an object that already is using a "Instance Object" as it's last modifier will instance the original object to keep things simple.

### What it does[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/instance_objects/#what-it-does "Permanent link")

For each selected object:

1.  Create a new object with the same mesh data as selected object. This is so you can still enter "Edit Mode" on the instance.
2.  Add [Instance Object](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/instance_object/)
     modifier and specify selected object.
3.  Select instance object and deselect original.

Select Original[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/instance_objects/#select-original "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Selects the original object(s) being instanced by selected object(s). This is useful when you wish to change modifier behavior on the original.

---

# normalMagic Add-on - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/#normalmagic-add-on)

normalMagic Add-on[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/#normalmagic-add-on "Permanent link")

==================================================================================================================

This add-on is included with the **Pro** version of normalMagic and makes working with certain modifiers easier.

*   [Install Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/install/#add-on)
    
*   [Preferences](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/preferences/)
    
*   [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![bpro panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)
    
    Set up booleans using the Boolean Pro [modifier](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
     with one click and convert existing booleans.
    
*   [Instance Objects](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/instance_objects/)
    
    * * *
    
    [![instance panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)
    
    Duplicate objects as instances using the Instance Object [modifier](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/instance_object/)
     with one click.

---

# Boolean Pro - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro)

Boolean Pro[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#boolean-pro "Permanent link")

================================================================================================================

[![bpro panel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/bpro_panel.png)

These tools make it easier to use the [Boolean Pro](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 modifier.

Top Panel Operators[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#top-panel-operators "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The top of the panel contains operators for all Boolean Pro modes.

*   ### How to use[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#how-to-use "Permanent link")
    
    [![bool diff](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/boolean_difference.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/boolean_difference.gif)
    
    **1.** Select cutter object(s).  
    **2.** Select the object you wish to cut.  
    **3.** Perform Boolean operation using the buttons.
    

### Operations[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#operations "Permanent link")

| Intersect | Union | Difference | Slice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [![Intersect](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_intersect.png) | [![Union](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_union.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_union.png) | [![Difference](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_diff.png) | [![Slice](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/booleans/bool_slice.gif) |

### Split[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#split "Permanent link")

The "Split" Operator will duplicate the object:

*   The original will get a **Difference** Boolean
*   The duplicate will get a **Intersect** Boolean. There is an option under [Cutter Behavior](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#cutter-behavior)
     to make the intersection object linked.

Modifier Settings[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#modifier-settings "Permanent link")

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section lets you change what settings the Boolean Pro modifiers get **before being added** (Settings for existing modifiers won't be updated).

All options in this section are covered on the [Boolean Pro modifier](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/boolean_pro/)
 page.

Cutter Behavior[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#cutter-behavior "Permanent link")

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

This section lets you choose what happens to objects when you add a Boolean.

*   **Display As.** Change the display mode of the cutter object:
    
    *   **Wireframe.** Display cutter as wireframe.
    *   **Bounds.** Display cutter as bounding box.
*   **Collection.** When enabled, specify the name of a collection to add cutter objects to. If the collection doesn't exist one will be created.
    
*   **Deselect.** Which objects get deselected after the boolean operation:
    *   **None.** Keep all objects selected.
    *   **Main Object.** Deselect the object that receives the modifier.
    *   **Cutters.** Deselect cutter object(s)
*   **Parent to Object.** Parent the cutter object(s) to the main object. This will transform them together when you move the main object.
*   **Link Split Mesh.** When performing the [Split](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#split)
     operation, link the intersected mesh to the original so editing one mesh edits the other too.
*   **Auto-Smooth.** Apply Blender's "Smooth By Angle" modifier to objects in Boolean operation if they don't have one already:
    *   **Cutters.** Apply smoothing to cutter object(s).
    *   **All.** Apply smoothing to all objects involved in the Boolean.
    *   **None.** Don't apply smoothing to any objects.
*   **Angle.** Angle to set auto-smoothing at when "Auto-Smooth" is turned on.

Convert Existing Booleans[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/boolean_pro/#convert-existing-booleans "Permanent link")

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Will convert all 'Boolean' modifiers on selected objects to 'Boolean Pro'. All relevant settings will be transferred.

---

# Instance Object - normalMagic

 

[Skip to content](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/instance_object/#instance-object)

Instance Object[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/instance_object/#instance-object "Permanent link")

=================================================================================================================================

Instances another object (after modifiers) to replace or append to the current objects geometry. This can be particularly useful for re-using details.

[![Instance](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/other_tools/instance.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/other_tools/instance.gif)
 Create duplicates of an object without needing to sync all modifier settings to the duplicates.

[![Instance Append](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/other_tools/instance_append.gif)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/other_tools/instance_append.gif)
 Append to and nest modular pieces. Such as these bolt inserts, these can be instanced themselves and used with [Surface Insert](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/mesh_tools/surface_insert/)
 on larger meshes.

Add-on

The normalMagic [Add-on](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/add-on/)
 has instance operators to make working with this modifier easier:

[![addonpanel](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/assets/add-on/instance_panel.png)

*   Create a linked duplicate of an object(s), replace all modifiers with **Instance Object**
*   Select original object being instanced

Options[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/instance_object/#options "Permanent link")

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Object.** Object to instance
*   **As Instance.** When turned off, will copy object's geometry. Turned on will instance the object [Official Documentation](https://docs.blender.org/manual/en/latest/modeling/geometry_nodes/instances.html)
    
*   **Replace / Append.** Replace will replace current objects geometry with that of the instance. Append will keep geometry and append the instanced object with additional transform options.

### Transform Instance[¶](https://spaghetmenot.github.io/normalmagic/1.1/other_tools/instance_object/#transform-instance "Permanent link")

When appending the instance, these settings allow you to transform the instance relative to the objects origin.
